# Campaign (G3)

The rules of a whole run in Godot: the sector map, shipyards, drydocks, events, rewards and the prize screen (add, strip or ransom), capture, retreat, saves, meta progression (renown, hull variants, admiral mastery, patron favour, Heat) and the run's end. It is a GDScript port of the browser game's `core.js` (run parts), `campaign_run.js`, `campaign_meta.js` and `campaign3d.js`, with the same names, order and maths.

## Files

- `campaign.gd`: the rules and the screen flow, one object.
- `data/campaign_tables.json`: every table the campaign reads, exported from the pinned JS files (`test/node/export_campaign.js`). Nothing is copied by hand. Boon effects and texts, which are code in `data.js`, are evaluated once per rarity.
- `test/`: the campaign parity test (below).

## How the screens work

Every place the JS game calls `show(html)` or `modal(html)`, the port sets:

- `page = {kind, data, acts}` (the full screen), or
- `modal_d = {kind, data, acts}` (a dialog on top; `null` when none).

`acts` lists the screen's buttons in the same order as the browser: `[act, arg, enabled]`. One call makes a click: `campaign.act("enterNode", "2-1")`. Nothing else changes campaign state, so a person, a test or the full-run bot all play the campaign the same way. The signal `changed` fires whenever the page or the modal changes; `toasts` holds short messages for the UI to show and clear.

Screen kinds: `title`, `admiral`, `aspect`, `map`, `fleet`, `shipyard`, `battle`, `legacy`, `help`, `end` (pages) and `intro`, `abandon`, `sell`, `drydock`, `event`, `event_result`, `victory`, `prize`, `salvage`, `mod`, `boon`, `relic`, `promote`, `retreat`, `sector` (modals).

## Battles

A fight node shows the `battle` page. Its buttons are `battle:win`, `battle:partial`, `battle:loss` and, except against a sector flagship, `c3retreat`.

- **Stub mode** (`battle_mode = "stub"`, the default): those buttons resolve the fight with the stub battle (`stub_start`, `stub_resolve`), which the JS harness copies line for line.
- **External mode** (`battle_mode = "external"`): set `battle_setup = func(run, cfg) -> Dictionary` to build the real battle (G1's rules: `startBattle(cfg)` reseeds the shared PRNG from `cfg.seed`, exactly as the game does) and return its `B`. The campaign then rolls the mid-battle event into `B.event` (as the game does after setup), shows the `battle` page and emits `battle_requested` with the config in `pending_battle`. The battle scene plays the fight and calls `campaign.finish_battle(result)` with `"win"`, `"loss"` or `"retreat"` (`can_retreat()` says whether the retreat button is allowed). The campaign reads the units of `B` (the JS field names: `ship`, `hp`, `maxHp`, `dead`, `dealt`, `kills`, `captured`, `killCause`, `killedBy`, `weapons`, `t`) for losses, experience, prizes and wrecks.

## The game shell hand-off

G2's `godot/main/campaign_host.gd` hosts the campaign (contract: `godot/main/README.md`). It creates `campaign.gd` (a RefCounted), sets `battle_mode = "external"` and `battle_setup`, draws the screens with `godot/ui/campaign/campaign_ui.gd`, and plays each battle when `battle_requested` fires.

- `battle_setup(run, cfg) -> B`: the campaign passes `cfg.campaign = true` (the rules roll the mid-battle event right after setup, as campaign3d.js does) and, in sectors at war, `cfg.event_war = true`. Stream B (B4) adds `cfg.nemesis` (the Nemesis fight: name, faction, weaknesses, adaptations, scars) and `cfg.verb` (a next-battle faction action); the rules apply both (`applyNemesis`, `applyVerb`).
- After the fight the host calls `finish_battle("win" | "loss" | "retreat", B)`; the campaign reads the units of `B` for losses, experience, prizes and wrecks, plus (B4) `B.usage` and unit `ev` (what the Nemesis learns), the unit with `nemFlag` (did his flagship die) and wing kills through `parent` (wing veterancy).

## Stream B campaign changes (port_notes.md steps 1 to 3, oracle c7b1a78)

Events with blue options (`needMet`, `needText`, `eventFlags`, no event repeats, 'quiet' once all are seen) and the new outcome kinds; the Nemesis (`newNemesis`, clock, `nemNodeIs` marks his fight on the map, `nemAfterBattle`, `nemLearn`, `nemAdapt` at each new sector); faction actions on the map (`factionVerb`); wing veterancy; legendary boons; boon reroll and decline; class refit tracks at shipyards (`buyTrack`); Corsair prize money; faction gates on prizes and towing. New buttons: `factionVerb`, `boonReroll` (arg `patron:minRarity`), `boonDecline`, `buyTrack` (arg the class name).
- `can_retreat()` says whether the battle may offer Retreat (never against a sector flagship).

## Stream B R42 Nemesis v2, R41 arc variants and step 4 (port_notes.md "R42 Nemesis v2" and "Step 4", oracle d8b47c6)

A new identity every run from the faction's own pools (`nemName`, `nemGender`, title, persona, two strength traits, a stored portrait id `nem_<faction>_<1..6>`), faction behaviour (`nemBeh`: clock pace, raid or tithe strikes on the map, flee, revive, feed, fast adaptation, reinforcements, duel, challenge), each logged in `RUN.nem.signatures` (`nemSignature`), scars with their cause (`scarKinds`) and an epithet each (`nemScar`), faction-named adaptations plus a faction special (`nemAdaptName`, `nemAdaptDesc`, `nemAdaptWhy`), what beat him last (`nemBeatOf`, `last_beat`), the Chorus feeding on your losses (`nemFeedLosses`), and the R41 arc hull unlock when he dies for good in sector III (`arcComplete`; arc variants cannot be bought). `cfg.nemesis` now also carries persona, traits, fed, riches, last_beat, g and met for the rules. Step 4 (R49): the weakness pool drops a weakness that cancels a drawn strength; a raid's credits go to `loot` and a towed ship to `held`, both returned when he dies; `gloat` after he beats you, `honed` for the Ithar who flees; the Deathless comeback only in sector III; the arc hull unlocks on any sector III win that does not bring him back; `nemBeatTeach` makes his next adaptation counter what beat him; `nemFoundMerge(B.nemFound)` keeps weaknesses found in battle; his fight uses his faction's fleet archetype; `nemSay` swaps pronouns for a she or an it; `nemWarnLine` says what the next win will do. `cfg.nemesis` also carries gloat, honed and beaten. Battle signatures: the browser's `nemSig` calls the campaign directly; in external battles the campaign records `B.nemSigs` kinds at `finish_battle`, before its own. `test/nem_scenarios.gd` against `test/node/nem_scenarios.js` (`run_nem_scenarios.sh`): the step 4 golden scenarios on the campaign side, identical record by record.

The war-room Nemesis card shows all of it in words (persona, strengths with their effect, his faction's behaviour line, the clock with half steps and its pace, fed, riches, the Deathless comeback, weaknesses and adaptations with their effects, what he remembers), and his face from the stored portrait id with one overlay per scar. The map's danger estimate counts his scars, adaptations, strengths, fed and riches, and the hover card lists what moved the number.

## The sector map look (R51, ui/campaign/map3d*.gd)

The 3D map is a star system round the Hollow Sun (Ryan, R51: a Sins of a Solar Empire 2 system). Presentation only; the rules, parity and the bot never see it.

- `map3d.gd`: layout, camera, input, labels, the hover card. The seven jumps sweep round the sun from left to right: column `c` sits at bearing `ANG0 - ANG_SPAN * c / 6`, a stop's orbit radius runs from `R_OUT` (row y = 0) to `R_IN` (y = 1), so lanes keep the old order and never cross more than before. Smooth zoom (wheel toward the cursor, + and -), pan (right or middle drag, WASD), Home or a double click resets; arriving in a sector or jumping resets the view.
- `map3d_system.gd`: the scene parts. The Hollow Sun, a black hole ringed by the accretion disc of the dead star (lore round 8; the same disc, lens and photon ring as G4's dead-star board, so map and battle agree): a black shadow with a thin bright photon ring, a disc tilted near edge-on with Doppler beaming (the side turning toward the camera brighter and whiter-blue, the receding side dimmer and redder, kept subtle), and the lensed image of its far side wrapping over the shadow and, thinner, under it, after real simulations (NASA Goddard 2019) (drawn as arcs on a camera-facing quad: G4's screen-space lens folded the disc at the lens edge on the map's free camera and would bend nearby stops), the system's only warm light, and a turning battlefield debris ring of rock, torn plates and girders, some smouldering., orbit lines, planets (rock, ocean, gas giant with rings, ice, lava) with an atmosphere rim lit from the sun and moons on their own orbits, stations, asteroid fields along the stop's orbit, nebula pockets (storms flicker), wreck fields, the sky and its parallax layers (near stars, nebula sheets below the chart). `LOOKS` holds the faction looks (colours only, no renames): Voidsworn black iron and ember, Hegemony grey and steel blue with lights pulsing in step, Corsairs soot, rust and dried blood, Chorus dark olive, ochre and bile yellow-green (round 4), Ithar clear crystal with rainbow edges, Deathless violet-black with a white-gold corona. The UI's faction name colours read the same table (`campaign_ui.fac_col`).
- `map3d_shaders.gd`: every shader, own uniforms and TIME only (no globals).
- `map3d_props.gd`: the stop objects at full finish (R51 round 2, Ryan: "the actual icons still look kind of low poly"), all procedural: an orbital station (spindle, habitat ring on spokes, docking arms, solar arrays, heat radiators, an antenna array, blinking running lights), construction gantries with a crane and floodlights round a hull being built (shipyard), a dock frame with clamps and work lights round a docked hull (drydock), a repair tender's lattice arms, floodlights and drones (field repairs), welding sparks, and asteroids from displaced icospheres with crater bowls and rims. Plating, seams, grit and lit windows come from the HULL shader's bump (no textures). Each stop reads as one clear object: no backdrop planets inside built stops, planet stops and their moons, asteroid fields and nebula pockets stay inside their own stop so nothing overlaps a neighbour at full-map zoom. Wrecks use A4's real death pieces (`gen/wreck/<CODE>_wreck.scn`) drifted apart with burning breaks. Planets have a thin atmosphere limb, a cloud layer and city lights on the night side. High: the disc's light casts soft shadows; every built stop has a faint cool fill so its plating reads away from the sun.
- Threat markers (round 3): a crisp holographic diamond over each fight in the danger band's colour (Easy green to Deadly red), the enemy faction's light at its centre, corner ticks, a scan line and a pulse; an elite adds a second outline and a gold crown bar. Replaces the lit gem (read as fantasy).
- Terms (lore round 8, R59): the map screen's own strings read `campaign_ui.TERMS`: core, signal, protocol, sync, and salvage for the relic item type (Opus's veto of V14). Texts that come from the browser game arrive through the re-exported tables and the oracle re-pin (f770e06: strings steps 10 and 12 and the battle-start fix). Classes show through `campaign.clsName()` (data.js `CLS_NAME`: War-barge, Colossus, Lattice-ship, Archive-ship); `cls` stays the lookup key. Faction crests: `ui/campaign/crest.gd` (the portraits helper's crest when it exists, else the bible's crest as vector art).
- `scenes/campaign/stops_probe.tscn -- <dir>`: close-ups of every stop kind and of the Hollow Sun at fixed angles (any map3d version), for before and after sets.
- Labels: under each stop its name, then the Nemesis and danger tags; the board shape and reward show on hover, keyboard focus or zoomed in, the place type (asteroid belt, ion storm...) only on hover (the hover card and the fight previews always carry them). Stacks that would overlap move down below each other; a stack that still touches another keeps to name and tags. Zoomed or panned, a stop whose labels would run under the briefing, the key, the fight cards or the side panel prints none. Rings of stops ahead take their kind's colour.
- The flat 2D map (`map_view.gd`) is retired (R51 audit): nothing set `use_3d_map` false, so it was unreachable.
- Fonts: headers use Michroma (a clean wide display face) and small caps use Oxanium SemiBold, both SIL Open Font License, bundled in `ui/campaign/fonts/` with their licences. The blackletter face is retired from the campaign screens.
- Quality: `campaign_ui.map_quality` ("high" or "low"; Low draws fewer stars, debris and cloud octaves, 2x MSAA). It starts from the game's Graphics setting (Low means low) and the map screen's "Map detail" button saves its own choice to `user://campaign_map.json`.
- Shots: `campaign.tscn -- mapshots <dir>` saves the map at fixed angles (start of a run, a hover card, a closer view, low quality, the start of sector II) for before and after sets.

## The story layer (godot/story/README.md)

- Screens: the admiral page, the sector I intro and sector change dialogs and the end page draw `story.campaign_line()`; the admiral card and the war-room panel show `story.titled_name()`; the fleet screen has "Rename flagship" (`story.open_rename()`); Progression has a "Career" tab button (`story.open_career()`); the admiral card shows the face from `godot/portraits/` (painting or crest). All guarded: without `godot/story/` or `godot/portraits/` nothing shows.
- Story arcs, behind a flag: `campaign.arcs_on` (default **false** until Ryan approves the arc text). When on, closing the sector I intro or a sector change dialog opens `arc_source(sector)` (the UI wires `story.arc_beat`) as an event card. Its choice runs the normal `eventFlags` and `applyOutcome`, completes no map node, adds the card's outcome line, saves, and calls `arc_chose_cb(card, key)` (`story.arc_chose`). At the end of a run that played beat 3, `arc_end_cb(won)` (`story.arc_end`). With the flag off, or on with no arc file for the faction, a run is identical step by step: `test/arc_hook_test.gd` checks both, plus the card flow with a test card.

## Rules access and tables

- The campaign reaches the battle rules only through `res://rules/battle_api.gd` (its `JSM`, the shared JS maths and PRNG, and a table-free rules object for the weapon trait effects). No copy of rules code is kept here.
- `load_tables` refuses to start when a table in `REQUIRED_TABLES` is missing, and `test/node/export_campaign.js` fails (exit 1, nothing written) when the oracle lacks any table the campaign scripts read.

## Saves

A fresh Godot save (browser saves are not carried over, R33): `user://voidsworn_campaign.json` plus a backup one save behind (`.bak`). Both are written atomically: whole to a `.tmp` file, flushed and size-checked, then renamed over the old file, so a crash mid-write leaves the old save or the new one, never half a file (`test/save_test.gd`). It holds the profile (`meta`), the saved run and a slim run log. Every non-integer number is written as its exact IEEE hex, because Godot's JSON reader can land one unit in the last place off. A damaged save falls back to the backup; the half that is sound is kept. Set `persist_on = true` and call `persist_load()` at start (the parity harness keeps saves in memory).

## Parity test

- **PC:** double-click `tools\pc\parity_campaign.bat` (also run at the end of `parity.bat`). It plays 30 seeded runs.
- **Linux or the cloud:** `GODOT=/path/to/godot sh godot/campaign/test/run_parity_campaign.sh [first] [last]`.

Node runs the pinned JS files (`test/oracle/`, unchanged) and plays whole runs, title to end screen, with a scripted player that clicks the buttons a player sees (read from the game's own HTML). Godot plays the same runs through `act()`. After every click both write the buttons on screen, the PRNG state and the campaign state in canonical form (run, map, profile, screen transients, the saved copy). `compare_campaign.js` names the first step and field that differ. There is no tolerance: numbers compare as exact doubles. The scripted player also reloads the page now and then (save, load, continue), which tests the save format and the reward-resume path.

What the harness leaves out, on both sides: the decorative title and map showcase battles (in the browser they draw from the rules PRNG, see the G3 report), telemetry, and the browser's back-fill of old run logs.
// G3 campaign parity: loads the root JS game files headless, unchanged, with no-op presentation calls,
// the same way godot/rules/test/node/golden.js and sim3d.js do. The browser page is replaced by small stubs; show() and
// modal() record the HTML they are given so the harness can read the buttons a player would see.
//
// What the harness replaces (presentation, not rules):
// - every 3D call (views, overlays, camera, banners, effects): no-ops;
// - the title and map "showcase" battles (setupShowcase, setupFleetShowcase): no-ops. In the browser these build a decorative
//   battle that draws from the rules PRNG; the Godot port does not, so both sides leave them out (see README, finding 1);
// - startBattle: the stub battle below (identical in GDScript, godot/campaign/battle_stub.gd), until real battles are hooked in;
// - Date.now() is 0 (ship ids x01, x02 ...), setTimeout runs nothing (toasts only), storage is empty.
'use strict';
const fs = require('fs'), path = require('path'), vm = require('vm');
const ORACLE = process.env.VS_ORACLE ? path.resolve(process.env.VS_ORACLE) : path.resolve(__dirname, '..', '..', '..', '..');
const FILES = ['data.js', 'core.js', 'battle.js', 'campaign_meta.js', 'campaign_run.js', 'campaign3d.js'];

// ui3d.js is presentation, but two of its tables reach the rules: FNAMES (ship names for non-Voidsworn fleets, read by
// core.js shipName when it exists, as it does in the browser) and TIPS (the battle onboarding tips, exported for the battle UI).
// Only those two blocks are evaluated, verbatim.
function uiTables() {
  const s = fs.readFileSync(path.join(ORACLE, 'ui3d.js'), 'utf8');
  const grab = n => { const i = s.indexOf('const ' + n + '='); if (i < 0) throw new Error('ui3d.js: no ' + n); const j = s.indexOf(';\n', i); return s.slice(i, j + 1); };
  return grab('FNAMES') + '\n' + grab('TIPS');
}
function load(extra) {
  Date.now = () => 0;
  global.require = require; global.Buffer = Buffer;
  const stub = new Proxy(function () {}, { get: (t, k) => (k === Symbol.toPrimitive ? () => '' : k === Symbol.iterator ? undefined : k === 'length' ? 0 : stub), apply: () => stub, set: () => true, construct: () => stub });
  const el = () => stub;
  global.window = { addEventListener() {} };
  global.document = { getElementById: el, querySelector: el, querySelectorAll: () => [], createElement: el, addEventListener() {}, body: stub, execCommand() { return false; } };
  global.localStorage = { getItem: () => null, setItem() {}, removeItem() {} };
  try { Object.defineProperty(global, "navigator", { value: {}, configurable: true, writable: true }); } catch (e) {}
  global.innerWidth = 1920; global.innerHeight = 1080;
  global.performance = { now: () => 0 };
  global.setTimeout = () => 0; global.setInterval = () => 0; global.clearInterval = () => {};
  const src = FILES.map(f => fs.readFileSync(path.join(ORACLE, f), 'utf8') + (f === 'battle.js' ? '\n' + uiTables() : '')).join('\n');
  const PRE = String.raw`
var G=new Proxy({mobile:false,quality:'high',fxReduced:false,shakeRed:false,sfxOn:true,cine:false,follow:true},{get:(o,k)=>k in o?o[k]:__stub});
function disposeAllViews(){} function clearOverlays(){} function hideEnd(){} function showIntro(){} function selectUnit(){} function banner(){} function prepBattle(){}
function refreshTop(){} function saveOpts(){} function setupBattle(){} function bRefresh(){} function buildBattleEnv(){} function hullMapPrefetch(){} function fxMark(){}
function survivorsP(){ return B?B.units.filter(u=>u.side==='player'&&!u.dead&&!u.isSq).length:0; }
var FRAMES={reset(){},summary(){ return null; }}, SFX={init(){},getVol(){ return 1; },setVol(){}}, SETUP={alt:true}, PLAYABLE=['voidsworn'];
function log(){} function fxText(){} function fxShield(){} function fxFlash(){}
`;
  global.__stub = stub;
  const POST = String.raw`
setupShowcase=function(){}; setupFleetShowcase=function(){};
`;
  vm.runInThisContext(PRE + src + '\n' + POST + '\n' + (extra || ''), { filename: 'campaign_bundle.js' });
}
module.exports = { load, ORACLE, FILES };

## The seats: who does what and how each is started (rewritten Nov 15 2026 from the Nov 14 update, R250; older seat text is in docs/archive/LOOP_history_2026-11-14.md)

As many threads as the Board can review well (R330; normally two or three); propose any big new thread to Ryan first (project instructions). Status files: codex_chain_*.txt, codex_lane_b.txt, codex_lane_c.txt in the PC root. Never start two runs on the same lane's tools: they share one repo folder and the second replaces the first's checkout. After a limit, a waiting run file (timeout until the reset). Results: board\issue-N\ (result.txt, last.md, log.txt, shots\). Image jobs need no GPU, 10 to 15 minutes per six pictures. rem Rules parity: the same five steps and diagnostics as run_parity.sh, with separate start /b workers.
rem Double-click, or parity.bat nopause from a watcher. Set PARITY_RUN_ROOT to keep detailed rules records.

if not defined GAME if exist "%HERE%..\..\godot\project.godot" set "GAME=%HERE%..\..\"

"%NODE%" "%GAME%\godot\rules\test\node\run_parity.js"

) else (
  echo FAIL: parity_campaign.bat not found next to parity.bat or in tools\pc\.
  set "RESULT=1"
  goto :end
)
if errorlevel 1 set "RESULT=1"
:end
echo.
if /i not "%~1"=="nopause" pause
endlocal & exit /b %RESULT%
@echo off
rem Voidsworn campaign parity check (G3): plays 30 seeded full runs (title to the end screen) twice, once with the game's own
rem JS campaign files at the repo root in Node (the oracle) and once with the Godot port headless, using
rem the same scripted player. Compares the campaign state after every click and prints PASS or FAIL with the first difference.
rem Double-click it, or run "parity_campaign.bat nopause" from a watcher job. parity.bat calls it at the end.
rem Works from Downloads\voidsworn_test\build\ (the repo's godot\ folder copied to build\godot\) or from a repo checkout.
setlocal enableextensions disabledelayedexpansion
set "HERE=%~dp0"
set "GAME="
set "RESULT=1"
if exist "%HERE%godot\project.godot" set "GAME=%HERE%"
if not defined GAME if exist "%HERE%..\..\godot\project.godot" set "GAME=%HERE%..\..\"
if not defined GAME ( echo FAIL: cannot find the Godot project ^(godot\project.godot next to this file or two folders up^). & goto :end )
set "PROJ=%GAME%godot\"
set "T=%PROJ%campaign\test\"
set "NODE="
set "GODOT="
for %%D in ("%GAME%.") do set "SEARCH=%%~fD"
rem Find the PC bundle above build or any board lane checkout, as in parity.bat.
:bundle
if not defined NODE if exist "%SEARCH%\node\node.exe" set "NODE=%SEARCH%\node\node.exe"
if not defined GODOT if exist "%SEARCH%\godot_path.txt" set /p GODOT=<"%SEARCH%\godot_path.txt"
for %%D in ("%SEARCH%\..") do set "NEXT=%%~fD"
if /i "%NEXT%"=="%SEARCH%" goto :bundle_done
set "SEARCH=%NEXT%"
goto :bundle
:bundle_done
if not defined NODE set "NODE=node"
rem Node guard (issue 357): the rules math targets V8 in Node 22; another major gives a false parity failure.
"%NODE%" -e "process.exit(process.versions.node.split('.')[0]==='22'?0:3)" >nul 2>&1
if errorlevel 1 ( echo FAIL: Node major 22 is required, use the bundled Node 22 at voidsworn_test\node\node.exe. & exit /b 1 )
if not defined GODOT if exist "%HERE%godot_path.txt" set /p GODOT=<"%HERE%godot_path.txt"
if not defined GODOT ( echo FAIL: no godot_path.txt found ^(it holds the path to the Godot console exe^). & goto :end )
set "LOG=%T%parity_campaign_log.txt"
if not exist "%T%golden" mkdir "%T%golden"
if not exist "%T%out" mkdir "%T%out"
echo Voidsworn campaign parity, %date% %time% > "%LOG%"
echo Project: %PROJ% >> "%LOG%"
echo Godot: %GODOT% >> "%LOG%"
set FAILS=0
setlocal enabledelayedexpansion
set "SEEDS="
for /L %%S in (1,1,30) do set "SEEDS=!SEEDS! %%S"
endlocal & set "SEEDS=%SEEDS%"

echo.
echo Campaign parity (G3)
echo [1/4] Campaign tables still match the root JS files...
"%NODE%" "%T%node\export_campaign.js" "%T%out\tables_check.json" >> "%LOG%" 2>&1
if not errorlevel 1 "%NODE%" -e "process.exit(require(process.argv[1]).sha256===require(process.argv[2]).sha256?0:1)" "%T%out\tables_check.json" "%PROJ%campaign\data\campaign_tables.json" >> "%LOG%" 2>&1
if errorlevel 1 ( echo   FAIL: campaign_tables.json is stale & set /a FAILS+=1 ) else ( echo   PASS )

echo [2/4] Playing 30 seeded runs in the JS game and in Godot...
for %%S in (%SEEDS%) do (
  "%NODE%" "%T%node\golden_campaign.js" %%S "%T%golden\c%%S.jsonl" >> "%LOG%" 2>&1
  if errorlevel 1 ( echo   FAIL: the JS run for seed %%S did not finish & set /a FAILS+=1 )
)
"%GODOT%" --headless --path "%PROJ%." --script res://campaign/test/parity_campaign.gd -- "%T%out" %SEEDS% >> "%LOG%" 2>&1
if errorlevel 1 ( echo   FAIL: the Godot runs did not finish & set /a FAILS+=1 )

echo [3/4] Comparing every click ^(buttons on screen, campaign state, saved copy, PRNG state^)...
for %%S in (%SEEDS%) do (
  "%NODE%" "%T%node\compare_campaign.js" "%T%golden\c%%S.jsonl" "%T%out\c%%S.jsonl" > "%T%out\cmp.txt" 2>&1
  if errorlevel 1 ( echo   run %%S: FAIL & type "%T%out\cmp.txt" & set /a FAILS+=1 ) else ( echo   run %%S: PASS )
  type "%T%out\cmp.txt" >> "%LOG%"
)

echo [4/4] Cross-twin save hand-off ^(fixed seed and step count, record by record^)...
"%NODE%" "%T%node\cross_twin_handoff_test.js" "%GODOT%" 1 40 >> "%LOG%" 2>&1
if errorlevel 1 ( echo   FAIL & set /a FAILS+=1 ) else ( echo   PASS )

echo.
if not "%FAILS%"=="0" (
  echo FAIL: %FAILS% check^(s^) differ. Details above and in "%LOG%"
  echo FAIL >> "%LOG%"
  goto :end
)
rem Keep diagnostics through the final comparison. On success remove only the files this run writes.
for %%S in (%SEEDS%) do (
  call :remove_output "golden\c%%S.jsonl"
  call :remove_output "out\c%%S.jsonl"
)
call :remove_output "out\tables_check.json"
call :remove_output "out\cmp.txt"
if not "%FAILS%"=="0" (
  echo FAIL: could not remove %FAILS% campaign parity output file^(s^). Details in "%LOG%"
  echo FAIL >> "%LOG%"
  goto :end
)
echo PASS: the Godot campaign matches the game on all 30 seeded runs, and the cross-twin save hand-off check passes.
echo PASS >> "%LOG%"
set "RESULT=0"
:end
echo.
if /i not "%~1"=="nopause" pause
endlocal & exit /b %RESULT%

:remove_output
if exist "%T%%~1" del /q "%T%%~1" >> "%LOG%" 2>&1
if not exist "%T%%~1" exit /b 0
echo Could not remove "%T%%~1" >> "%LOG%"
set /a FAILS+=1
exit /b 1

class CampaignProbe extends RefCounted:
	var RUN = {"pos": "a", "fac": "voidsworn", "map": {"cols": [[{"id": "a"}]], "edges": [
		["a", "b"], ["a", "c"], ["b", "d"], ["c", "e"], ["d", "f"], ["e", "f"]]}}

	var acts: Array = []
	func availableNodes() -> Array:
		if RUN["pos"] == null: return RUN["map"]["cols"][0].map(func(n): return n["id"])
		var next: Array = []
		for e in RUN["map"]["edges"]:
			if e[0] == RUN["pos"]: next.append(e[1])
		return next
	func jt(v) -> bool: return bool(v) if v != null else false
	func act(action, id):
		acts.append([action, id]); RUN["pos"] = id

#!/bin/sh
# G3 campaign parity, cloud / Linux twin of tools/pc/parity_campaign.bat. Same steps, same PASS/FAIL.
#   GODOT=/path/to/godot sh godot/campaign/test/run_parity_campaign.sh [first_seed] [last_seed]     (default seeds 1 to 24)
# 1. checks the campaign tables still match the root game files (re-exports and compares the checksum);
# 2. Node plays each seeded run with the root JS files (golden/), Godot plays the same runs (out/);
# 3. compares them step by step and prints PASS, or the first step and field that differ.
cd "$(dirname "$0")"; T=$(pwd); P=$(cd ../.. && pwd); G="${GODOT:-godot}"; F=0; A=${1:-1}; Z=${2:-24}
# Node guard (issue 357): the rules math targets V8 in Node 22; another major gives a false parity failure.
NV=$(node -p "process.versions.node" 2>/dev/null)
case "$NV" in
  22.*) : ;;
  *) echo "FAIL: Node major 22 is required, found ${NV:-no node}; use the bundled Node 22 at voidsworn_test/node/node.exe."; exit 1 ;;
esac
mkdir -p golden out
node node/export_campaign.js out/tables_check.json > /dev/null || F=$((F+1))
if node -e "const a=require('./out/tables_check.json').sha256,b=require('../data/campaign_tables.json').sha256; process.exit(a===b?0:1)"; then echo "  tables: PASS (match the root game files)"; else echo "  tables: FAIL (campaign_tables.json is stale: run node godot/campaign/test/node/export_campaign.js)"; F=$((F+1)); fi
SEEDS=$(seq "$A" "$Z")
for s in $SEEDS; do node node/golden_campaign.js "$s" "golden/c$s.jsonl" > /dev/null || { echo "  seed $s: the JS run did not finish"; F=$((F+1)); }; done
"$G" --headless --path "$P" --script res://campaign/test/parity_campaign.gd -- "$T/out" $SEEDS > out/godot.log 2>&1
grep -E "SCRIPT ERROR|ERROR:" out/godot.log | head -5
N=0
for s in $SEEDS; do N=$((N+1))
  if [ ! -f "out/c$s.jsonl" ]; then echo "  seed $s: FAIL (Godot wrote nothing; see godot/campaign/test/out/godot.log)"; F=$((F+1)); continue; fi
  if node node/compare_campaign.js "golden/c$s.jsonl" "out/c$s.jsonl" > out/cmp.txt; then echo "  seed $s: $(cat out/cmp.txt)"; rm -f "out/c$s.jsonl" "golden/c$s.jsonl"; else echo "  seed $s: $(cat out/cmp.txt)"; F=$((F+1)); fi
  done
# issue 374: one cross-twin check of the save hand-off at a fixed seed and step count. The one-line ruling on the
# by-design split and the record-by-record comparison are inside the script.
if node node/cross_twin_handoff_test.js "$G" 1 40; then :; else F=$((F+1)); fi
if [ $F -eq 0 ]; then echo "PASS: the Godot campaign matches the game on all $N seeded runs, and the cross-twin save hand-off check passes."; else echo "FAIL: $F check(s) differ."; exit 1; fi

"""Picture labels and review evidence. Pillow is already used by the sheet tools.

New renders call stamp_file immediately after saving. Compositors use Review.open
and Review.save: input revisions survive crops, resizing and later sheet runs.
The covered pixels live in the sidecar so alpha measurements see the original.
"""
import argparse
import base64
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from functools import lru_cache
import hashlib
import io
import json
import shutil
from pathlib import Path
import re
import subprocess
import sys
from urllib.parse import quote, unquote, urlsplit

if __name__ != '__main__' or sys.argv[1:2] != ['context']:
    from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageSequence

STAGES = ('sheet built', 'paint proof', 'installed in game')
EXTENSIONS = {'.png', '.jpg', '.jpeg', '.webp', '.gif'}
ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]


def digest(data):
    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()


def sidecar(path):
    return Path(str(path) + '.provenance.json')


def git(repo, *args):
    return subprocess.check_output(['git', '-C', str(repo), *args], stderr=subprocess.PIPE)


@lru_cache(maxsize=8)
def revision(repo):
    commit = git(repo, 'rev-parse', 'HEAD').decode().strip()
    if not re.fullmatch(r'[a-f0-9]{40,64}', commit):
        raise ValueError('picture source has no full git commit')
    changes = git(repo, 'diff', '--binary', 'HEAD')
    # Include new source files as well as tracked edits. Do not hash PC assets.
    for name in git(repo, 'ls-files', '--others', '--exclude-standard', '-z').decode().split('\0'):
        if name and name != 'CODEX_TASK.md' and Path(name).suffix in {'.py', '.gd', '.js', '.ps1', '.bat'}:
            changes += name.encode() + b'\0' + (Path(repo) / name).read_bytes()
    return dict(source_commit=commit, source_dirty=bool(changes),
                source_diff_sha256=digest(changes) if changes else '')


def text_lines(record):
    lines = [record['picture_name']]
    for src in record['sources']:
        rev = src['source_commit'][:12]
        if 'source_commit_date' in src:
            rev = 'built from ' + rev
        elif src['source_dirty']:
            rev += ' + working ' + src['source_diff_sha256'][:8]
        line = rev + ' | ' + src['stage']
        if line not in lines:
            lines.append(line)
    lines.append(record['stage'] + ' | ' + record['date'])
    return lines


def strip_for(record, size):
    # One bitmap alphabet for Python and Godot, independent of installed fonts.
    glyphs = json.loads((Path(__file__).with_name('picture_font.json')).read_text())
    scale = 2 if size[0] >= 1000 else 1
    width = size[0] // scale
    lines = []
    for text in text_lines(record):
        while text:
            end = max(1, (width - 12) // 6)
            lines.append(text[:end])
            text = text[end:]
    height = len(lines) * 13 + 10
    if height * scale > size[1]:
        raise ValueError('picture is too small for a readable provenance strip')
    strip_width = min(width, max(len(t) * 6 for t in lines) + 12)
    strip = Image.new('RGB', (strip_width, height), '#10151e')
    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(strip)
    draw.line((0, 0, strip_width, 0), fill='#e8b56b')
    for row, text in enumerate(lines):
        for col, char in enumerate(text):
            for y, bits in enumerate(glyphs.get(char, glyphs['?'])):
                for x in range(6):
                    if bits & (1 << (5 - x)):
                        draw.point((6 + col * 6 + x, 4 + row * 13 + y), fill='#f1e8d9')
    return strip.resize((strip.width * scale, height * scale), Image.Resampling.NEAREST)


def summary(record):
    keys = ('picture_name', 'source_commit', 'source_dirty', 'source_diff_sha256',
            'stage', 'date', 'image_sha256', 'sources')
    result = {k: record[k] for k in keys}
    if 'source_commit_date' in record:
        result['source_commit_date'] = record['source_commit_date']
    return result


def verify(path):
    path = Path(path)
    record = json.loads(sidecar(path).read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    if record.get('schema') != 1 or record.get('picture_name') != path.name:
        raise ValueError('wrong picture name or provenance schema: ' + str(path))
    if record.get('stage') not in STAGES:
        raise ValueError('missing or invalid picture stage: ' + str(path))
    datetime.strptime(record['date'], '%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ')
    for src in [record] + record['sources']:
        if not re.fullmatch(r'[a-f0-9]{40,64}', src['source_commit']) or src['stage'] not in STAGES:
            raise ValueError('invalid source revision/stage: ' + str(path))
        if src['source_dirty'] and not re.fullmatch(r'[a-f0-9]{64}', src['source_diff_sha256']):
            raise ValueError('working revision lacks its digest: ' + str(path))
        if 'source_commit_date' in src:
            datetime.strptime(src['source_commit_date'], '%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ')
            if src['source_dirty'] or src['source_diff_sha256']:
                raise ValueError('build revision has a working-tree state: ' + str(path))
    if not record['sources'] or record['image_sha256'] != digest(path.read_bytes()):
        raise ValueError('picture changed after stamping: ' + str(path))
    with Image.open(path) as image:
        box = record['stamp_box']
        frames = [f.copy() for f in ImageSequence.Iterator(image)]
        if len(frames) != len(record['strip_sha256']):
            raise ValueError('picture frame count changed: ' + str(path))
        for frame, expected in zip(frames, record['strip_sha256']):
            strip = frame.convert('RGB').crop(box)
            if digest(strip.tobytes()) != expected or strip.size != tuple(record['strip_size']):
                raise ValueError('missing burned-in strip: ' + str(path))
            template = strip_for(record, image.size)
            if template.size != strip.size or digest(template.tobytes()) != record['stamp_template_sha256']:
                raise ValueError('invalid stamp bounds: ' + str(path))
            # JPEG and GIF quantize pixels. The text still has to be present.
            from PIL import ImageChops, ImageStat
            if max(ImageStat.Stat(ImageChops.difference(strip, template)).mean) > 18:
                raise ValueError('provenance text is absent from the picture: ' + str(path))

const fs = require('fs'), os = require('os'), path = require('path');
const { spawn } = require('child_process');
const T = path.resolve(__dirname, '..'), P = path.resolve(T, '../..');

let root, failures = 0;
function say(line) {
  console.log(line);
  fs.appendFileSync(path.join(root, 'parity_log.txt'), line + '\n');
}
function positive(name, fallback) {
  const value = process.env[name] === undefined ? fallback : Number(process.env[name]);
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(value) || value < 1) throw new Error(`${name} must be a positive integer`);
  return value;
}
function present(file) { return fs.existsSync(file) && fs.statSync(file).size > 0; }
function read(file) { return fs.existsSync(file) ? fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8').replace(/\r\n/g, '\n') : ''; }
function result(r) { return (r.error || r.signal || String(r.code)) + (r.timeout ? ', timeout' : ''); }
function cleanExit(r) { return r.code === 0 && !r.timeout && !r.error; }
async function waitForAll(tasks) {
  const failed = (await Promise.allSettled(tasks)).find(r => r.status === 'rejected');
  if (failed) throw failed.reason;
}
function paused() {
  if (!windows || !process.env.USERPROFILE) return false;
  const file = path.join(process.env.USERPROFILE, 'Downloads', 'voidsworn_test', 'gpu_go.txt');
  try {
    return /^GPU not cleared: paused by the Board\b/i.test(fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8').replace(/^\uFEFF/, ''));
  } catch (e) {
    if (e.code === 'ENOENT') return false;
    throw e;
  }
}
async function withRunRoot(check) {
  const chosen = process.env.PARITY_RUN_ROOT;
  const temp = path.resolve(process.env.RUNNER_TEMP || os.tmpdir());
  const dir = chosen ? path.resolve(chosen) : fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(temp, 'voidsworn-parity-'));
  // Keep caller-selected output and GitHub's parity evidence available for inspection.
  const owned = !chosen && process.env.GITHUB_ACTIONS !== 'true';
  const created = owned ? fs.lstatSync(dir) : null;
  let code = 1;
  try {
    fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
    if (fs.readdirSync(dir).length) throw new Error(`PARITY_RUN_ROOT must be empty: ${dir}`);
    code = await check(dir);
  } finally {
    if (owned) {
      try {
        if (paused()) console.log(`Parity scratch kept during the PC pause: ${dir}`);
        else {
          const current = fs.lstatSync(dir, { throwIfNoEntry: false });
          if (current) {
            if (!current.isDirectory() || current.dev !== created.dev || current.ino !== created.ino) throw new Error('directory was replaced');
            fs.rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true, maxRetries: 3, retryDelay: 100 });
            console.log(`Parity scratch removed: ${dir}`);
          }
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.error(`FAIL: could not clean up parity scratch at ${dir}: ${e.message}`);
        code = 1;
      }
    }
  }
  return code;
}
function isolated(dir) {
  fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
  // Windows Godot uses APPDATA rather than XDG_DATA_HOME. Isolate both, including the engine log.
  return { ...process.env, XDG_DATA_HOME: dir, APPDATA: dir };
}
function execute(exe, args, log, env = process.env, seconds = 300, start = false) {
  return new Promise(resolve => {
    const fd = fs.openSync(log, 'w');
    let child, timeout = false, error;
    try {
      if (windows && start) {
        // start /b launches Godot separately; /wait keeps its exit code and lifetime attached to the worker.
        const quote = s => {
          if (/["\r\n%]/.test(s)) throw new Error('unsupported character in Windows worker path');
          return `"${s}"`;
        };
        const script = log + '.cmd';
        fs.writeFileSync(script, '@echo off\r\nstart "" /b /wait ' + [exe, ...args].map(quote).join(' ') + '\r\nexit /b %errorlevel%\r\n');
        child = spawn(process.env.ComSpec || 'cmd.exe', ['/d', '/s', '/c', `call "${script}"`], { env, windowsHide: true, windowsVerbatimArguments: true, stdio: ['ignore', fd, fd] });
      } else child = spawn(exe, args, { env, windowsHide: true, stdio: ['ignore', fd, fd] });
    } catch (e) {
      fs.closeSync(fd); resolve({ code: null, error: e.message }); return;
    }
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      timeout = true;
      if (windows) {
        // Stop only this owned worker and its child, then wait for close before inspecting records.
        const killer = spawn('taskkill.exe', ['/pid', String(child.pid), '/t', '/f'], { windowsHide: true, stdio: ['ignore', fd, fd] });
        // If Windows refuses termination, retain the wait handle. Killing only cmd would orphan Godot
        // and let it write records while comparisons or diagnostic reruns are already in progress.
        killer.on('error', e => { error = 'watchdog could not stop worker tree: ' + e.message; });
        killer.on('close', code => { if (code !== 0) error = 'watchdog could not stop worker tree, taskkill exit ' + code; });
      } else child.kill('SIGKILL');
    }, seconds * 1000);
    child.on('error', e => { error = e.message; });
    child.on('close', (code, signal) => {
      clearTimeout(timer); fs.closeSync(fd); resolve({ code, signal, timeout, error });
    });
  });
}
function gd(args, log, env, seconds = 300, start = false) {
  return execute(godot, ['--headless', '--path', P, '--script', 'res://rules/test/parity_main.gd', '--', ...args], log, env, seconds, start);
}
function js(file, args, log) { return execute(process.execPath, [path.join(__dirname, file), ...args], log); }
function checked(label, ok, details = '') {
  say(`  ${label}: ${ok ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'}${details ? ' (' + details + ')' : ''}`);
  if (!ok) failures++;
}

# Campaign test record (G3)

The evidence behind "G3 campaign rules: done, with parity". Every run below can be repeated with the command next to it.
Newest first. Each row names the commit the run used and where its log lives.

## What the oracle is

- The game's own JS campaign files, pinned unchanged: `godot/campaign/test/oracle/`, copied from **8e81d75** (Nov 1 2026: stream B step 9b, the RAMP sector 2 budget, the last rules change; before that f770e06, steps 10 to 12, the lore round 8 strings and the campaign battle-start fix; before that d8b47c6, steps 0 to 4, with one local comment fix). See `oracle/README.md`.
- The campaign rules in Godot last changed at **ae6073b** (port of stream B step 4, oracle d8b47c6). Step 9b is a number change inside the RAMP table only, so it reaches Godot through the re-exported tables with no code change. Everything after that in `godot/campaign/` is the test harness or notes; the screens (`godot/ui/campaign/`) never change campaign state (every button calls `campaign.act()`), so map and panel work cannot move parity.
- The tables (`data/campaign_tables.json`) are exported from the oracle by `node/export_campaign.js`; every parity run first re-exports them and checks the checksum.

The short runs (7, 11, 12, 13, 24, 27) are runs the scripted player abandons or loses early; they still check the title, admiral, aspect, intro and first fights.

## Scenarios

### Parity runs (seeds 1 to 30)

Each seed is a whole run, title screen to the end screen, played by the same scripted player in the JS game and in Godot. After every click both sides write the buttons on screen, the PRNG state and the full campaign state (run, map, profile, screen transients, the saved copy); `node/compare_campaign.js` names the first step and field that differ, with no tolerance (exact doubles). The player also reloads the page now and then (save, load, continue).

What the 24-seed set exercised (`node node/coverage.js`): 3873 steps; 16 runs won, 8 lost or abandoned; deepest sector S1 5, S2 0, S3 19. Clicks: enterNode 426, takePerk 355, prizeSet 264, battle 221 (win 192, partial 21, loss 8), takeSalvage 212 (credits 144, refit 68), closeModal 168, equip 135, prizeConfirm 120, prizeStep 120, takeBoon 118, sellShip 112, takeRelic 103, upgradeMk 100, leaveYard 96, salvageStep 91, continueRun 89, buyHull 83, buyMod 78, sellConfirm 51, buyTrack 49, reload 47, unequip 44, saveQuit 42, backMap 39, progTab 39, yardFleet 39, boonDecline 36, eventChoice 36, fleetScreen 36, pickFaction 36, factionVerb 34, repair 33, boonReroll 32, buyVariant 32, newRunScreen 32, title 30, repairAll 28, pickAdmiral 26, end 24, legacy 24, pickAspect 24, start 24, startRun 24, takeMod 21, optHeat 14, c3retreat 12 (yes 6, no 6), fleetFromModal 6, help 6, optAlt 6, optCmd 6.

### Nemesis scenarios (`run_nem_scenarios.sh`, 64 records)

1. Deathless: a sector II kill scars him; the first sector III kill brings him back once; the second kills him and unlocks the arc hull.
2. Corsair: 10 credits and a crippled ship; a full clock tows her; he flees and keeps her; killed in sector III he gives her and the loot back; the tithe with 200 credits.
3. Ithar: he flees: honed, no riches; his battle config.
4. Chorus: feeding and its cap; a loss with your flagship dead; fast adaptation with its reason; his battle config.
5. Voidsworn: beaten with lances, his next adaptation is the counter.
6. Weaknesses found in battle merge (duplicates and unknown ids dropped); pronouns for a she and an it.
7. Every faction's warning line at a full clock, and the tithe.

### Other checks

- `save_test.gd`: atomic writes (tmp file, size check, rename), the backup one save behind, a damaged save falling back to the backup.
- `arc_hook_test.gd`: the story arc hook off, on with no arc file, and on with a test card (a run with the flag off or with no arc file is identical step by step).

{'PIL': True, 'cairosvg': False, 'svglib': False, 'reportlab': False, 'fitz': False, 'clr': False}
def open_picture(path, required=False):
    """Restore only the pixels hidden by the strip, for crops and measurements."""
    path = Path(path)
    record = verify(path) if sidecar(path).exists() or required else None
    with Image.open(path) as original:
        image = original.copy()
    if record:
        patch = Image.open(io.BytesIO(base64.b64decode(record['covered_pixels'][0])))
        image = image.convert(patch.mode)
        image.paste(patch, tuple(record['stamp_box'][:2]))
    return image


def save_picture(image, path, stage, sources=None, repo=None, **options):
    if stage not in STAGES:
        raise ValueError('unknown picture stage: ' + stage)
    path = Path(path)
    if path.suffix.lower() not in EXTENSIONS:
        raise ValueError('unsupported review image: ' + str(path))
    source_list = []
    for record in sources or []:
        for src in record['sources']:
            if src not in source_list:
                source_list.append(src)
    if not source_list:
        source_list = [dict(revision(str(Path(repo or ROOT).resolve())), stage=stage)]
    record = dict(source_list[0], schema=1, picture_name=path.name, stage=stage,
                  date=datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ'),
                  sources=source_list, inputs=[summary(s) for s in sources or []])
    strip = strip_for(record, image.size)
    box = [0, image.height - strip.height, strip.width, image.height]
    frames = [image] + list(options.pop('append_images', []))
    covered = []
    stamped = []
    for frame in frames:
        frame = frame.copy().convert('RGBA' if 'A' in frame.getbands() and path.suffix.lower() not in {'.jpg', '.jpeg'} else 'RGB')
        if frame.size != image.size:
            raise ValueError('animation frames must have the same size')
        buf = io.BytesIO()
        frame.crop(box).save(buf, format='PNG')
        covered.append(base64.b64encode(buf.getvalue()).decode('ascii'))
        frame.paste(strip, tuple(box[:2]))
        stamped.append(frame)
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    if len(stamped) > 1:
        options.update(save_all=True, append_images=stamped[1:])
    stamped[0].save(path, **options)
    with Image.open(path) as saved:
        strips = [digest(f.convert('RGB').crop(box).tobytes()) for f in ImageSequence.Iterator(saved)]
    record.update(stamp_box=box, strip_size=list(strip.size), strip_sha256=strips,
                  stamp_template_sha256=digest(strip.tobytes()),
                  covered_pixels=covered, image_sha256=digest(path.read_bytes()))
    sidecar(path).write_text(json.dumps(record, indent=2) + '\n', encoding='utf-8', newline='\n')
    return record


def stamp_file(path, stage, repo=None, new_render=False):
    path = Path(path)
    if sidecar(path).exists() and not new_render:
        # Never turn a cached old capture into a new revision.
        return verify(path)
    with Image.open(path) as image:
        frames = [f.convert('RGBA') for f in ImageSequence.Iterator(image)]
        options = {}
        if image.format == 'GIF':
            options.update(duration=image.info.get('duration', 100), loop=image.info.get('loop', 0))
        elif image.format == 'JPEG':
            options.update(quality=95)
    return save_picture(frames[0], path, stage, repo=repo, append_images=frames[1:], **options)


class Review:
def publish_report(repo, out, destination):
    """Copy only the linked, validated shots. Rewrite the report before staging."""
    repo, out = Path(repo).resolve(), Path(out).resolve()
    report = out / 'last.md'
    if not report.exists():
        return
    body = report.read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig')
    copies, replacements = [], []
    matches = sorted(picture_target_matches(body), key=lambda match: (match.start(1), -match.end(1)))
    previous_end = 0
    for match in matches:
        start, end = match.span(1)
        if start < previous_end:
            continue  # A complete link already covers this bare path match.
        previous_end = end
        if match[1].startswith('<') and match[1].endswith('>'):
            start, end = start + 1, end - 1
        old = body[start:end]
        path_text, *suffix = re.split(r'(?=[?#])', old, maxsplit=1)
        name = unquote(path_text)
        candidate = Path(name)
        absolute = candidate.is_absolute()
        if name.replace('\\', '/').startswith('//') or (not absolute and re.match(r'(?i)[a-z]+:', name)):
            continue  # Leave UNC paths and URLs to check_body.
        if not absolute:
            candidate = out / candidate
        try:
            resolved = candidate.resolve()
        except (OSError, RuntimeError, ValueError):
            continue
        try:
            relative = resolved.relative_to(out / 'shots')
        except ValueError:
            if not absolute:
                continue
            try:
                relative = resolved.relative_to(repo)
            except ValueError:
                continue
        else:
            verify(candidate)
            dest = repo / destination / relative
            dest.resolve().relative_to(repo)
            copies.append((candidate, dest))
            relative = dest.relative_to(repo)
        new = quote(relative.as_posix()) + ''.join(suffix)
        if old != new:
            replacements.append((start, end, old, new))
    for start, end, _, new in reversed(replacements):
        body = body[:start] + new + body[end:]
    for source, dest in copies:
        dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        shutil.copyfile(source, dest)
        shutil.copyfile(sidecar(source), sidecar(dest))
    check_body(repo, body, tracked=False)
    report.write_text(body, encoding='utf-8')
    for _, _, old, new in replacements:
        print(f'Rewrote picture link: {old} -> {new}')

# ---------------- threads, errors and the hard stop ----------------
# Every public function is a thin wrapper: lock, run the body (_i_<name>), check, unlock. So step() or threat_map() may run on a
# worker thread while the scene calls view(), ui_info() or anything else: the other caller waits for the lock instead of reading
# half-changed state. A runtime error inside the rules returns from the failing function with a default value and execution goes
# on (GDScript has no exceptions), so the wrapper always reaches its unlock; the error itself is caught by a Logger that counts
# script errors raised in res://rules/ during the call.
# Hard stop (on by default in debug builds and for bot runs: set hard_stop = true): a rules script error or a NOT PORTED rule
# (rules.gd np() or an unported maths branch in jsm.gd) during any call halts the battle: phase becomes "halted", view().halt says
# why, an event {t: "halt", why} is emitted, and every command and step() does nothing until the next new_battle. With hard_stop
# off (release builds) the error is still logged and counted, and play goes on.

+extends SceneTree

+const CampaignScript = preload("res://campaign/campaign.gd")
+const JSM = preload("res://rules/battle_api.gd").JSM
+const SECTORS := 3000
+const REST_TYPES := ["shipyard", "drydock", "field"]
+
+func _init() -> void:
+	var campaign = CampaignScript.new()
+	var err: String = campaign.load_tables()
+	if not err.is_empty():
+		printerr(err)
+		quit(1)
+		return
+	var eligible := 0
+	var two_exits := 0
+	var same_type := 0
+	var first_repeat := ""
+	for seed_value in range(1, SECTORS + 1):
+		var sector := 1 + (seed_value - 1) % 3
+		JSM.rnd_seed(seed_value)
+		var map: Dictionary = campaign.genSector(sector)
+		var problem := layout_problem(map)
+		if not problem.is_empty():
+			printerr("FAIL: seed %d, sector %d: %s" % [seed_value, sector, problem])
+			quit(1)
+			return
+		var next_types := {}
+		for edge in map["edges"]:
+			var target: PackedStringArray = edge[1].split("-")
+			if not next_types.has(edge[0]): next_types[edge[0]] = []
+			next_types[edge[0]].append(map["cols"][int(target[0])][int(target[1])]["type"])
+		for col in range(5):
+			for stop in map["cols"][col]:
+				var exits: Array = next_types[stop["id"]]
+				if col > 0:
+					eligible += 1
+					if exits.size() == 2: two_exits += 1
+				if exits.size() == 2 and exits[0] == exits[1]:
+					same_type += 1
+					if first_repeat.is_empty():
+						first_repeat = "seed %d, stop %s, type %s" % [seed_value, stop["id"], exits[0]]
+	var rate := 100.0 * two_exits / eligible
+	print("Sector routes: %d sectors, %d/%d stops in columns 1 to 4 with two exits (%.2f%%), %d same-type forks" % [SECTORS, two_exits, eligible, rate, same_type])
+	if same_type > 0: printerr("First same-type fork: " + first_repeat)
+	var passed := rate >= 80.0 and same_type == 0
+	print("PASS: sector route choices" if passed else "FAIL: expected at least 80% two-exit stops and zero same-type forks")
+	quit(0 if passed else 1)
+
+func layout_problem(map: Dictionary) -> String:
+	var cols: Array = map["cols"]
+	if cols.size() != 7 or cols[0].size() != 1 or cols[6].size() != 1 or cols[6][0]["type"] != "boss":
+		return "expected seven columns with a single start and a single boss at column 6"
+	if cols[5].is_empty() or not cols[5].all(func(stop): return stop["type"] in REST_TYPES):
+		return "expected only rest stops in column 5"
+	if not cols[5].any(func(stop): return stop["type"] in ["shipyard", "drydock"]):
+		return "expected a shipyard or drydock in column 5"
+	var columns := {}
+	for col in cols:
+		for stop in col: columns[stop["id"]] = stop["col"]
+	var incoming := {}
+	var outgoing := {}
+	for edge in map["edges"]:
+		var source: String = edge[0]
+		var target: String = edge[1]
+		if not columns.has(source) or not columns.has(target) or columns[target] != columns[source] + 1:
+			return "edge %s to %s must join consecutive columns" % [source, target]
+		if not outgoing.has(source): outgoing[source] = {}
+		if outgoing[source].has(target): return "duplicate edge %s to %s" % [source, target]
+		outgoing[source][target] = true
+		incoming[target] = true
+	for id in columns:
+		if columns[id] > 0 and not incoming.has(id): return "unreachable stop " + id
+		if columns[id] < 6 and not outgoing.has(id): return "dead end at " + id
+	return ""

+extends SceneTree
+
+const CampaignScript = preload("res://campaign/campaign.gd")
+const JSM = preload("res://rules/battle_api.gd").JSM
+const SECTORS := 3000
+const REST_TYPES := ["shipyard", "drydock", "field"]
+
+func _init() -> void:
+	var campaign = CampaignScript.new()
+	var err: String = campaign.load_tables()
+	if not err.is_empty():
+		printerr(err)
+		quit(1)
+		return
+	var eligible := 0
+	var two_exits := 0
+	var same_type := 0
+	var first_repeat := ""
+	for seed_value in range(1, SECTORS + 1):
+		var sector := 1 + (seed_value - 1) % 3
+		JSM.rnd_seed(seed_value)
+		var map: Dictionary = campaign.genSector(sector)
+		var problem := layout_problem(map)
+		if not problem.is_empty():
+			printerr("FAIL: seed %d, sector %d: %s" % [seed_value, sector, problem])
+			quit(1)
+			return
+		var next_types := {}
+		for edge in map["edges"]:
+			var target: PackedStringArray = edge[1].split("-")
+			if not next_types.has(edge[0]): next_types[edge[0]] = []
+			next_types[edge[0]].append(map["cols"][int(target[0])][int(target[1])]["type"])
+		for col in range(5):
+			for stop in map["cols"][col]:
+				var exits: Array = next_types[stop["id"]]
+				if col > 0:
+					eligible += 1
+					if exits.size() == 2: two_exits += 1
+				if exits.size() == 2 and exits[0] == exits[1]:
+					same_type += 1
+					if first_repeat.is_empty():
+						first_repeat = "seed %d, stop %s, type %s" % [seed_value, stop["id"], exits[0]]
+	var rate := 100.0 * two_exits / eligible
+	print("Sector routes: %d sectors, %d/%d stops in columns 1 to 4 with two exits (%.2f%%), %d same-type forks" % [SECTORS, two_exits, eligible, rate, same_type])
+	if same_type > 0: printerr("First same-type fork: " + first_repeat)
+	var passed := rate >= 80.0 and same_type == 0
+	print("PASS: sector route choices" if passed else "FAIL: expected at least 80% two-exit stops and zero same-type forks")
+	quit(0 if passed else 1)
+
+func layout_problem(map: Dictionary) -> String:
+	var cols: Array = map["cols"]
+	if cols.size() != 7 or cols[0].size() != 1 or cols[6].size() != 1 or cols[6][0]["type"] != "boss":
+		return "expected seven columns with a single start and a single boss at column 6"
+	if cols[5].is_empty() or not cols[5].all(func(stop): return stop["type"] in REST_TYPES):
+		return "expected only rest stops in column 5"
+	if not cols[5].any(func(stop): return stop["type"] in ["shipyard", "drydock"]):
+		return "expected a shipyard or drydock in column 5"
+	var columns := {}
+	for col in cols:
+		for stop in col: columns[stop["id"]] = stop["col"]
+	var incoming := {}
+	var outgoing := {}
+	for edge in map["edges"]:
+		var source: String = edge[0]
+		var target: String = edge[1]
+		if not columns.has(source) or not columns.has(target) or columns[target] != columns[source] + 1:
+			return "edge %s to %s must join consecutive columns" % [source, target]
+		if not outgoing.has(source): outgoing[source] = {}
+		if outgoing[source].has(target): return "duplicate edge %s to %s" % [source, target]
+		outgoing[source][target] = true
+		incoming[target] = true
+	for id in columns:
+		if columns[id] > 0 and not incoming.has(id): return "unreachable stop " + id
+		if columns[id] < 6 and not outgoing.has(id): return "dead end at " + id
+	return ""

# Campaign parity test (G3)

Node runs the root JS game files (`data.js`, `core.js`, `battle.js`, `campaign_meta.js`, `campaign_run.js`, `campaign3d.js`) and Godot runs `campaign.gd`; both play the same seeded runs with the same scripted player and write one record per click. There is no pinned copy; set `VS_ORACLE` only when the game files sit in another tree. `FNAMES` and `TIPS` are read from the root `ui3d.js`. `node/compare_campaign.js` stops at the first step and field that differ. There is no tolerance: numbers compare as exact doubles in canonical form (keys sorted, null and undefined left out, integers plain, other numbers as IEEE hex).

- PC: `tools\pc\parity_campaign.bat` (30 runs). Linux: `GODOT=... sh godot/campaign/test/run_parity_campaign.sh [first] [last]`.
- Files: `node/env.js` (loads the oracle headless), `node/golden_campaign.js` (the JS side), `parity_campaign.gd` (the Godot side), `node/compare_campaign.js`, `node/export_campaign.js` (the tables), `node/coverage.js` (what the runs exercised).

## Other campaign checks

- `run_nem_scenarios.sh`: the Nemesis scenarios (Deathless comeback, Corsair loot and towed ship, Ithar honed, Chorus feeding and gloat, beat-teaching, weaknesses found in battle, pronouns, every faction's warning line) in the JS game and in Godot, compared record by record.
- `arc_hook_test.gd`: the story arc hook (off, on with no arc file, on with a test card).
- `save_test.gd`: atomic saves, the backup, a damaged save loading the backup.

## When a record is written

- Once at the start (the title screen), then after every click the scripted player makes, and after every simulated page reload.
- The record is written after the click's whole effect, including every screen it opens.

## What every record holds

| Field | What it is |
|---|---|
| `act`, `arg` | the click (or `reload`, `start`, `end`) |
| `acts` | every button on the screen now, in order, with enabled or disabled: on the JS side read from the HTML the game produced |
| `rng` | the state of the game's PRNG (mulberry32, `RNG_S`), the only random stream the campaign rules use |
| `run` | the whole run object except its map (fleet, ships, captains, credits, relics, boons, reward chain, stats, reserve, ...) |
| `map` | the sector map (nodes with their encounter, seed, reward, shipyard stock; edges; condition; region; biome) |
| `meta` | the profile (renown, unlocks, mastery, favour, variants, stats, options) except the telemetry log |
| `saved` | the saved copy of the run (which also checks when saves happen) |
| `t` | everything else that can change what happens next: the JS `window._*` transients (reward config, patron reward, relic choice, wrecks, boon and refit offers, aspect offer), the open event (id and the options shown, which depend on the faction's wording), the shipyard node, the picked admiral and faction, the run options (alternate fleet, alternate Command, Heat), the progression tab, the ship id counter (`UID`), whether the run has ended, and the battle in progress (seed, round, result, partial, campaign and finished flags, the mid-battle event, and every unit's id, side, hull, hp, dead, captured, fled, kills, damage, kill cause and killer) |

Each part is stored in full when it changes and as a 16-hex SHA-256 every step.

## What is left out, on purpose

- **Telemetry** (`META.tel`): it only fed the browser's back-fill of old run logs, which a Godot profile never runs (`backfilled` and `wonFix41` are set on both sides).
- **The page and toast text**: wording is checked by eye; the buttons are compared.
- **The function to call after a promotion** (`window._promoteAfter`): its effect shows in the next screen's buttons.
- **Presentation**: the decorative title and map showcase battles (in the browser they reseed the rules PRNG; both sides leave them out, see the G3 report), the 3D map, toasts, sounds.
- **Real battles**: both sides use the same stub battle (`stub_start`, `stub_resolve` in `campaign.gd`, copied line for line in `golden_campaign.js`). The battle rules have their own parity test (`godot/rules/test/`).

static func ssort(a: Array, cmp: Callable) -> Array:
	var n := a.size()
	if n < 2: return a
	var src := a.duplicate(); var dst := src.duplicate(); var width := 1
	while width < n:
		var i := 0
		while i < n:
			var mid := mini(i + width, n); var end := mini(i + 2 * width, n); var l := i; var r := mid; var k := i
			while l < mid and r < end:
				if float(cmp.call(src[r], src[l])) < 0.0:
					dst[k] = src[r]; r += 1
				else:
					dst[k] = src[l]; l += 1
				k += 1
			while l < mid:
				dst[k] = src[l]; l += 1; k += 1
			while r < end:
				dst[k] = src[r]; r += 1; k += 1
			i += 2 * width
		var tmp := src; src = dst; dst = tmp; width *= 2

+'use strict';
+
+// node draw_maps.js <output-directory> [before-revision]
+// Draws the route diagrams as SVG. The review copies are rasterized and stamped separately.
+const fs = require('node:fs');
+const os = require('node:os');
+const path = require('node:path');
+const { execFileSync } = require('node:child_process');
+
+const repo = path.resolve(__dirname, '../../..');
+const harness = path.join(repo, 'godot/campaign/test/node/env.js');
+const { FILES } = require(harness);
+const font = require(path.join(repo, 'tools/picture_font.json'));
+const examples = [1, 2, 3];
+const colors = {
+  battle: '#e6ba8c', elite: '#f69082', event: '#b3a7ef',
+  shipyard: '#8fd9c4', drydock: '#82cfe9', field: '#d5db98', boss: '#f7af6b',
+};
+const originX = 112;
+const columnStep = 176;
+const ink = '#edf0f7';
+const faint = '#a8b6cd';
+const single = '#627897';
+const choice = '#79caff';
+const repeat = '#fc9f87';
+
+function git(...args) {
+  return execFileSync('git', ['-C', repo, ...args]);
+}
+
+function loadMaps(oracle) {
+  const sample = `
+    function sampleMap(seed) {
+      rndSeed(seed);
+      return genSector(1 + (seed - 1) % 3);
+    }
+    console.log(JSON.stringify(${JSON.stringify(examples)}.map(sampleMap)));
+  `;
+  const loader = 'require(process.argv[1]).load(process.argv[2])';
+  return JSON.parse(execFileSync(process.execPath, ['-e', loader, harness, sample], {
+    env: { ...process.env, VS_ORACLE: oracle },
+  }));
+}
+
+function text(label, x, y, scale, fill, align = 'left') {
+  // The bundled bitmap font becomes paths, so a CPU SVG rasterizer needs no fonts.
+  if (align === 'center') x -= label.length * 3 * scale;
+  let pixels = '';
+  for (const [index, letter] of [...label].entries()) {
+    const glyph = font[letter] || font['?'];
+    for (const [row, bits] of glyph.entries()) {
+      for (let col = 0; col < 6; col++) {
+        if (bits & (1 << (5 - col))) pixels += `M${index * 6 + col} ${row}h1v1h-1z`;
+      }
+    }
+  }
+  return `<path d="${pixels}" transform="translate(${x} ${y}) scale(${scale})" fill="${fill}"/>`;
+}
+
+function rect(x, y, w, h, radius, fill, stroke = 'none') {
+  return `<rect x="${x}" y="${y}" width="${w}" height="${h}" rx="${radius}" fill="${fill}" stroke="${stroke}"/>`;
+}
+
+function panel(map, top, label) {
+  const stops = new Map(map.cols.flat().map(stop => [stop.id, stop]));
+  const exits = new Map([...stops.keys()].map(id => [id, []]));
+  for (const [from, to] of map.edges) exits.get(from).push(stops.get(to));
+  const eligible = map.cols.slice(1, 5).flat();
+  const two = eligible.filter(stop => exits.get(stop.id).length === 2).length;
+  const sameType = id => {
+    const next = exits.get(id);
+    return next.length === 2 && next[0].type === next[1].type;
+  };
+  const repeated = [...stops.keys()].filter(sameType).length;
+  const y = stop => Math.round(top + 49 + 142 * stop.y);
+  const x = stop => originX + columnStep * stop.col;
+  const elements = [
+    rect(32, top, 1216, 198, 16, '#141f32', '#2a3a55'),
+    text(label, 51, top + 14, 2, ink),
+    text(`COLUMNS 1-4: ${two}/${eligible.length} TWO EXITS   |   ${repeated} SAME-TYPE FORKS`, 635, top + 14, 2, faint),
+  ];
+  for (const [from, to] of map.edges) {
+    const a = stops.get(from), b = stops.get(to);
+    const edgeColor = sameType(from) ? repeat : exits.get(from).length >= 2 ? choice : single;
+    elements.push(`<path d="M${x(a) + 51} ${y(a)}C${x(a) + 90} ${y(a)} ${x(b) - 90} ${y(b)} ${x(b) - 51} ${y(b)}" fill="none" stroke="${edgeColor}" stroke-width="2"/>`);
+  }
+  for (const stop of stops.values()) {
+    const color = colors[stop.type];
+    elements.push(rect(x(stop) - 51, y(stop) - 14, 102, 28, 7, '#101827', color));
+    elements.push(text(stop.type.toUpperCase(), x(stop), y(stop) - 11, 2, color, 'center'));
+  }
+  return elements.join('\n');
+}
+
+function diagram(seed, base, before, after) {
+  const code = `ROUTE-1176-${String(seed).padStart(2, '0')}`;
+  const elements = [
+    rect(0, 0, 1280, 700, 0, '#0c1423'),
+    text(code, 34, 24, 3, ink),
+    text(`SECTOR ${seed}   |   SEED ${seed}`, 34, 62, 2, faint),
+    text('SECTOR MAP ROUTE CHOICES', 930, 29, 2, faint),
+  ];
+  const headings = ['0 START', '1', '2', '3', '4', '5 REST', '6 BOSS'];
+  for (const [i, label] of headings.entries()) {
+    elements.push(text(label, originX + columnStep * i, 98, 2, ink, 'center'));
+  }
+  elements.push(panel(before, 128, `BEFORE / MAIN ${base.slice(0, 8).toUpperCase()}`));
+  elements.push(panel(after, 350, 'AFTER / ISSUE 1176 WORKING TREE'));
+  for (const [i, [label, color]] of [['SINGLE EXIT', single], ['CHOICE', choice], ['SAME-TYPE FORK', repeat]].entries()) {
+    const x = 34 + i * 234;
+    elements.push(rect(x, 580, 28, 3, 1, color));
+    elements.push(text(label, x + 38, 570, 2, ink));
+  }
+  return `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="1280" height="700">\n${elements.join('\n')}\n</svg>\n`;
+}
+
+const [, , output, ref = 'origin/main'] = process.argv;
+if (!output) throw new Error('Usage: node draw_maps.js <output-directory> [before-revision]');
+const base = git('rev-parse', '--verify', `${ref}^{commit}`).toString().trim();
+const temp = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'voidsworn-route-before-'));
+try {
+  for (const file of [...FILES, 'ui3d.js']) fs.writeFileSync(path.join(temp, file), git('show', `${base}:${file}`));
+  const before = loadMaps(temp);
+  const after = loadMaps(repo);
+  fs.mkdirSync(output, { recursive: true });
+  for (const [index, seed] of examples.entries()) {
+    const name = `route-1176-${String(seed).padStart(2, '0')}.svg`;
+    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(output, name), diagram(seed, base, before[index], after[index]));
+  }
+} finally {
+  for (const file of [...FILES, 'ui3d.js']) fs.rmSync(path.join(temp, file), { force: true });
+  fs.rmdirSync(temp);
+}

+extends SceneTree
+
+const CampaignScript = preload("res://campaign/campaign.gd")
+const JSM = preload("res://rules/battle_api.gd").JSM
+const SECTORS := 3000
+const REST_TYPES := ["shipyard", "drydock", "field"]
+
+func _init() -> void:
+	var campaign = CampaignScript.new()
+	var err: String = campaign.load_tables()
+	if not err.is_empty():
+		printerr(err)
+		quit(1)
+		return
+	var eligible := 0
+	var two_exits := 0
+	var same_type := 0
+	var first_repeat := ""
+	for seed_value in range(1, SECTORS + 1):
+		var sector := 1 + (seed_value - 1) % 3
+		JSM.rnd_seed(seed_value)
+		var map: Dictionary = campaign.genSector(sector)
+		var problem := layout_problem(map)
+		if not problem.is_empty():
+			printerr("FAIL: seed %d, sector %d: %s" % [seed_value, sector, problem])
+			quit(1)
+			return
+		var next_types := {}
+		for edge in map["edges"]:
+			var target: PackedStringArray = edge[1].split("-")
+			if not next_types.has(edge[0]): next_types[edge[0]] = []
+			next_types[edge[0]].append(map["cols"][int(target[0])][int(target[1])]["type"])
+		for col in range(5):
+			for stop in map["cols"][col]:
+				var exits: Array = next_types[stop["id"]]
+				if col > 0:
+					eligible += 1
+					if exits.size() == 2: two_exits += 1
+				if exits.size() == 2 and exits[0] == exits[1]:
+					same_type += 1
+					if first_repeat.is_empty():
+						first_repeat = "seed %d, stop %s, type %s" % [seed_value, stop["id"], exits[0]]
+	var rate := 100.0 * two_exits / eligible
+	print("Sector routes: %d sectors, %d/%d stops in columns 1 to 4 with two exits (%.2f%%), %d same-type forks" % [SECTORS, two_exits, eligible, rate, same_type])
+	if same_type > 0: printerr("First same-type fork: " + first_repeat)
+	var passed := rate >= 80.0 and same_type == 0
+	print("PASS: sector route choices" if passed else "FAIL: expected at least 80% two-exit stops and zero same-type forks")
+	quit(0 if passed else 1)
+
+func layout_problem(map: Dictionary) -> String:
+	var cols: Array = map["cols"]
+	if cols.size() != 7 or cols[0].size() != 1 or cols[6].size() != 1 or cols[6][0]["type"] != "boss":
+		return "expected seven columns with a single start and a single boss at column 6"
+	if cols[5].is_empty() or not cols[5].all(func(stop): return stop["type"] in REST_TYPES):
+		return "expected only rest stops in column 5"
+	if not cols[5].any(func(stop): return stop["type"] in ["shipyard", "drydock"]):
+		return "expected a shipyard or drydock in column 5"
+	var columns := {}
+	for col in cols:
+		for stop in col: columns[stop["id"]] = stop["col"]
+	var incoming := {}
+	var outgoing := {}
+	for edge in map["edges"]:
+		var source: String = edge[0]
+		var target: String = edge[1]
+		if not columns.has(source) or not columns.has(target) or columns[target] != columns[source] + 1:
+			return "edge %s to %s must join consecutive columns" % [source, target]
+		if not outgoing.has(source): outgoing[source] = {}
+		if outgoing[source].has(target): return "duplicate edge %s to %s" % [source, target]
+		outgoing[source][target] = true
+		incoming[target] = true
+	for id in columns:
+		if columns[id] > 0 and not incoming.has(id): return "unreachable stop " + id
+		if columns[id] < 6 and not outgoing.has(id): return "dead end at " + id
+	return ""

+'use strict';
+
+// node draw_maps.js <output-directory> [before-revision]
+// Draws the route diagrams as SVG. The review copies are rasterized and stamped separately.
+const fs = require('node:fs');
+const os = require('node:os');
+const path = require('node:path');
+const { execFileSync } = require('node:child_process');
+
+const repo = path.resolve(__dirname, '../../..');
+const harness = path.join(repo, 'godot/campaign/test/node/env.js');
+const { FILES } = require(harness);
+const font = require(path.join(repo, 'tools/picture_font.json'));
+const examples = [1, 2, 3];
+const colors = {
+  battle: '#e6ba8c', elite: '#f69082', event: '#b3a7ef',
+  shipyard: '#8fd9c4', drydock: '#82cfe9', field: '#d5db98', boss: '#f7af6b',
+};
+const originX = 112;
+const columnStep = 176;
+const ink = '#edf0f7';
+const faint = '#a8b6cd';
+const single = '#627897';
+const choice = '#79caff';
+const repeat = '#fc9f87';
+
+function git(...args) {
+  return execFileSync('git', ['-C', repo, ...args]);
+}
+
+function loadMaps(oracle) {
+  const sample = `
+    function sampleMap(seed) {
+      rndSeed(seed);
+      return genSector(1 + (seed - 1) % 3);
+    }
+    console.log(JSON.stringify(${JSON.stringify(examples)}.map(sampleMap)));
+  `;
+  const loader = 'require(process.argv[1]).load(process.argv[2])';
+  return JSON.parse(execFileSync(process.execPath, ['-e', loader, harness, sample], {
+    env: { ...process.env, VS_ORACLE: oracle },
+  }));
+}
+
+function text(label, x, y, scale, fill, align = 'left') {
+  // The bundled bitmap font becomes paths, so a CPU SVG rasterizer needs no fonts.
+  if (align === 'center') x -= label.length * 3 * scale;
+  let pixels = '';
+  for (const [index, letter] of [...label].entries()) {
+    const glyph = font[letter] || font['?'];
+    for (const [row, bits] of glyph.entries()) {
+      for (let col = 0; col < 6; col++) {
+        if (bits & (1 << (5 - col))) pixels += `M${index * 6 + col} ${row}h1v1h-1z`;
+      }
+    }
+  }
+  return `<path d="${pixels}" transform="translate(${x} ${y}) scale(${scale})" fill="${fill}"/>`;
+}
+
+function rect(x, y, w, h, radius, fill, stroke = 'none') {
+  return `<rect x="${x}" y="${y}" width="${w}" height="${h}" rx="${radius}" fill="${fill}" stroke="${stroke}"/>`;
+}
+
+function panel(map, top, label) {
+  const stops = new Map(map.cols.flat().map(stop => [stop.id, stop]));
+  const exits = new Map([...stops.keys()].map(id => [id, []]));
+  for (const [from, to] of map.edges) exits.get(from).push(stops.get(to));
+  const eligible = map.cols.slice(1, 5).flat();
+  const two = eligible.filter(stop => exits.get(stop.id).length === 2).length;
+  const sameType = id => {
+    const next = exits.get(id);
+    return next.length === 2 && next[0].type === next[1].type;
+  };
+  const repeated = [...stops.keys()].filter(sameType).length;
+  const y = stop => Math.round(top + 49 + 142 * stop.y);
+  const x = stop => originX + columnStep * stop.col;
+  const elements = [
+    rect(32, top, 1216, 198, 16, '#141f32', '#2a3a55'),
+    text(label, 51, top + 14, 2, ink),
+    text(`${two}/${eligible.length} TWO EXITS IN COLS 1-4   |   SAME-TYPE FORKS: ${repeated}`, 580, top + 14, 2, faint),
+  ];
+  for (const [from, to] of map.edges) {
+    const a = stops.get(from), b = stops.get(to);
+    const edgeColor = sameType(from) ? repeat : exits.get(from).length >= 2 ? choice : single;
+    elements.push(`<path d="M${x(a) + 51} ${y(a)}C${x(a) + 90} ${y(a)} ${x(b) - 90} ${y(b)} ${x(b) - 51} ${y(b)}" fill="none" stroke="${edgeColor}" stroke-width="2"/>`);
+  }
+  for (const stop of stops.values()) {
+    const color = colors[stop.type];
+    elements.push(rect(x(stop) - 51, y(stop) - 14, 102, 28, 7, '#101827', color));
+    elements.push(text(stop.type.toUpperCase(), x(stop), y(stop) - 11, 2, color, 'center'));
+  }
+  return elements.join('\n');
+}
+
+function diagram(seed, base, before, after) {
+  const code = `ROUTE-1176-${String(seed).padStart(2, '0')}`;
+  const elements = [
+    rect(0, 0, 1280, 700, 0, '#0c1423'),
+    text(code, 34, 24, 3, ink),
+    text(`SECTOR ${seed}   |   SEED ${seed}`, 34, 62, 2, faint),
+    text('SECTOR MAP ROUTE CHOICES', 930, 29, 2, faint),
+  ];
+  const headings = ['0 START', '1', '2', '3', '4', '5 REST', '6 BOSS'];
+  for (const [i, label] of headings.entries()) {
+    elements.push(text(label, originX + columnStep * i, 98, 2, ink, 'center'));
+  }
+  elements.push(panel(before, 128, `BEFORE / MAIN ${base.slice(0, 8).toUpperCase()}`));
+  elements.push(panel(after, 350, 'AFTER / ISSUE 1176 WORKING TREE'));
+  for (const [i, [label, color]] of [['SINGLE EXIT', single], ['CHOICE', choice], ['SAME-TYPE FORK', repeat]].entries()) {
+    const x = 34 + i * 234;
+    elements.push(rect(x, 580, 28, 3, 1, color));
+    elements.push(text(label, x + 38, 570, 2, ink));
+  }
+  return `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="1280" height="700">\n${elements.join('\n')}\n</svg>\n`;
+}
+
+const [, , output, ref = 'origin/main'] = process.argv;
+if (!output) throw new Error('Usage: node draw_maps.js <output-directory> [before-revision]');
+const base = git('rev-parse', '--verify', `${ref}^{commit}`).toString().trim();
+const temp = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'voidsworn-route-before-'));
+try {
+  for (const file of [...FILES, 'ui3d.js']) fs.writeFileSync(path.join(temp, file), git('show', `${base}:${file}`));
+  const before = loadMaps(temp);
+  const after = loadMaps(repo);
+  fs.mkdirSync(output, { recursive: true });
+  for (const [index, seed] of examples.entries()) {
+    const name = `route-1176-${String(seed).padStart(2, '0')}.svg`;
+    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(output, name), diagram(seed, base, before[index], after[index]));
+  }
+} finally {
+  for (const file of [...FILES, 'ui3d.js']) fs.rmSync(path.join(temp, file), { force: true });
+  fs.rmdirSync(temp);
+}
