diff --git a/godot/campaign/test/sector_routes_test.gd b/godot/campaign/test/sector_routes_test.gd
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..fa82ab84e5f9fefecbd5d18b26453e737f69bb63

+++ b/godot/campaign/test/sector_routes_test.gd

+extends SceneTree
+
+const CampaignScript = preload("res://campaign/campaign.gd")
+const JSM = preload("res://rules/battle_api.gd").JSM
+const SECTORS := 3000
+const REST_TYPES := ["shipyard", "drydock", "field"]
+
+func _init() -> void:
+	var campaign = CampaignScript.new()
+	var err: String = campaign.load_tables()
+	if not err.is_empty():
+		printerr(err)
+		quit(1)
+		return
+	var eligible := 0
+	var two_exits := 0
+	var same_type := 0
+	var first_repeat := ""
+	for seed_value in range(1, SECTORS + 1):
+		var sector := 1 + (seed_value - 1) % 3
+		JSM.rnd_seed(seed_value)
+		var map: Dictionary = campaign.genSector(sector)
+		var problem := layout_problem(map)
+		if not problem.is_empty():
+			printerr("FAIL: seed %d, sector %d: %s" % [seed_value, sector, problem])
+			quit(1)
+			return
+		var types_by_id := {}
+		for col in map["cols"]:
+			for stop in col: types_by_id[stop["id"]] = stop["type"]
+		var next_types := {}
+		for edge in map["edges"]:
+			if not next_types.has(edge[0]): next_types[edge[0]] = []
+			next_types[edge[0]].append(types_by_id[edge[1]])
+		for col in range(5):
+			for stop in map["cols"][col]:
+				var exits: Array = next_types[stop["id"]]
+				if col > 0:
+					eligible += 1
+					if exits.size() == 2: two_exits += 1
+				if exits.size() == 2 and exits[0] == exits[1]:
+					same_type += 1
+					if first_repeat.is_empty():
+						first_repeat = "seed %d, stop %s, type %s" % [seed_value, stop["id"], exits[0]]
+	var rate := 100.0 * two_exits / eligible
+	print("Sector routes: %d sectors, %d/%d stops in columns 1 to 4 with two exits (%.2f%%), %d same-type forks" % [SECTORS, two_exits, eligible, rate, same_type])
+	if same_type > 0: printerr("First same-type fork: " + first_repeat)
+	var passed := rate >= 80.0 and same_type == 0
+	print("PASS: sector route choices" if passed else "FAIL: expected at least 80% two-exit stops and zero same-type forks")
+	quit(0 if passed else 1)
+
+func layout_problem(map: Dictionary) -> String:
+	var cols: Array = map["cols"]
+	if cols.size() != 7 or cols[0].size() != 1 or cols[6].size() != 1 or cols[6][0]["type"] != "boss":
+		return "expected seven columns with a single start and a single boss at column 6"
+	if cols[5].is_empty() or not cols[5].all(func(stop): return stop["type"] in REST_TYPES):
+		return "expected only rest stops in column 5"
+	if not cols[5].any(func(stop): return stop["type"] in ["shipyard", "drydock"]):
+		return "expected a shipyard or drydock in column 5"
+	var columns := {}
+	for col in cols:
+		for stop in col: columns[stop["id"]] = stop["col"]
+	var incoming := {}
+	var outgoing := {}
+	for edge in map["edges"]:
+		var source: String = edge[0]
+		var target: String = edge[1]
+		if not columns.has(source) or not columns.has(target) or columns[target] != columns[source] + 1:
+			return "edge %s to %s must join consecutive columns" % [source, target]
+		if not outgoing.has(source): outgoing[source] = {}
+		if outgoing[source].has(target): return "duplicate edge %s to %s" % [source, target]
+		outgoing[source][target] = true
+		incoming[target] = true
+	for id in columns:
+		if columns[id] > 0 and not incoming.has(id): return "unreachable stop " + id
+		if columns[id] < 6 and not outgoing.has(id): return "dead end at " + id
+	return ""

+# Sector map route choices
+
+Every stop in columns 1 to 4 offers two routes to different types of stops. The opening stop links to every stop in column 1. Column 5 offers rest stops, and every rest stop leads to the boss in column 6.
+
+The check generated 3,000 sectors with seeds 1 to 3,000, rotating through sectors 1, 2 and 3. It counted stops with exactly two exits in columns 1 to 4 and checked every two-way fork for repeated stop types, including forks at the starting stop.
+
+| Source | Two-exit stops, columns 1 to 4 | Same-type forks |
+|---|---:|---:|
+| Before, main `da81dd82` | 14,778 of 35,905 (41.16%) | 3,463 |
+| After, issue 1176 | 35,905 of 35,905 (100%) | 0 |
+
+The brief cites 58.7% for main. The table reports what this check measured. All stops remained reachable in both samples.
+
+Campaign parity passed for seeds 1 through 30, and the first 40 steps of seed 1 matched in the separate save hand-off check. The [campaign parity record](../../../godot/campaign/test/RECORD.md) has the table checksum and save counts.
+
+## Before and after
+
+Each diagram shows the same seed and sector before and after the change. The labels on the stops give their types. Red lines mark repeated types at a two-way fork. Blue lines mark choices.
+
+![ROUTE-1176-01, sector 1, seed 1](../issue-1176-codex/route-1176-01.png)
+
+![ROUTE-1176-02, sector 2, seed 2](../issue-1176-codex/route-1176-02.png)
+
+![ROUTE-1176-03, sector 3, seed 3](../issue-1176-codex/route-1176-03.png)
+
+Run `node docs/shots/route_choice_1176/draw_maps.js <scratch-directory> da81dd82` from the repo root to redraw the SVG diagrams. To rerun the sector check, use `godot --headless --path godot --script res://campaign/test/sector_routes_test.gd`.

+'use strict';
+
+// node draw_maps.js <output-directory> [before-revision]
+// Draws the route diagrams as SVG. The review copies are rasterized and stamped separately.
+const fs = require('node:fs');
+const os = require('node:os');
+const path = require('node:path');
+const { execFileSync } = require('node:child_process');
+
+const repo = path.resolve(__dirname, '../../..');
+const harness = path.join(repo, 'godot/campaign/test/node/env.js');
+const { FILES } = require(harness);
+const font = require(path.join(repo, 'tools/picture_font.json'));
+const examples = [1, 2, 3];
+const colors = {
+  battle: '#e6ba8c', elite: '#f69082', event: '#b3a7ef',
+  shipyard: '#8fd9c4', drydock: '#82cfe9', field: '#d5db98', boss: '#f7af6b',
+};
+const originX = 112;
+const columnStep = 176;
+const ink = '#edf0f7';
+const faint = '#a8b6cd';
+const single = '#627897';
+const choice = '#79caff';
+const repeat = '#fc9f87';
+
+function git(...args) {
+  return execFileSync('git', ['-C', repo, ...args]);
+}
+
+function loadMaps(oracle) {
+  const sample = `
+    function sampleMap(seed) {
+      rndSeed(seed);
+      return genSector(1 + (seed - 1) % 3);
+    }
+    console.log(JSON.stringify(${JSON.stringify(examples)}.map(sampleMap)));
+  `;
+  const loader = 'require(process.argv[1]).load(process.argv[2])';
+  return JSON.parse(execFileSync(process.execPath, ['-e', loader, harness, sample], {
+    env: { ...process.env, VS_ORACLE: oracle },
+  }));
+}
+
+function text(label, x, y, scale, fill, align = 'left') {
+  // The bundled bitmap font becomes paths, so a CPU SVG rasterizer needs no fonts.
+  if (align === 'center') x -= label.length * 3 * scale;
+  let pixels = '';
+  for (const [index, letter] of [...label].entries()) {
+    const glyph = font[letter] || font['?'];
+    for (const [row, bits] of glyph.entries()) {
+      for (let col = 0; col < 6; col++) {
+        if (bits & (1 << (5 - col))) pixels += `M${index * 6 + col} ${row}h1v1h-1z`;
+      }
+    }
+  }
+  return `<path d="${pixels}" transform="translate(${x} ${y}) scale(${scale})" fill="${fill}"/>`;
+}
+
+function rect(x, y, w, h, radius, fill, stroke = 'none') {
+  return `<rect x="${x}" y="${y}" width="${w}" height="${h}" rx="${radius}" fill="${fill}" stroke="${stroke}"/>`;
+}
+
+function panel(map, top, label) {
+  const stops = new Map(map.cols.flat().map(stop => [stop.id, stop]));
+  const exits = new Map([...stops.keys()].map(id => [id, []]));
+  for (const [from, to] of map.edges) exits.get(from).push(stops.get(to));
+  const eligible = map.cols.slice(1, 5).flat();
+  const two = eligible.filter(stop => exits.get(stop.id).length === 2).length;
+  const sameType = id => {
+    const next = exits.get(id);
+    return next.length === 2 && next[0].type === next[1].type;
+  };
+  const repeated = [...stops.keys()].filter(sameType).length;
+  const y = stop => Math.round(top + 49 + 142 * stop.y);
+  const x = stop => originX + columnStep * stop.col;
+  const elements = [
+    rect(32, top, 1216, 198, 16, '#141f32', '#2a3a55'),
+    text(label, 51, top + 14, 2, ink),
+    text(`${two}/${eligible.length} TWO EXITS IN COLS 1-4   |   SAME-TYPE FORKS: ${repeated}`, 580, top + 14, 2, faint),
+  ];
+  for (const [from, to] of map.edges) {
+    const a = stops.get(from), b = stops.get(to);
+    const edgeColor = sameType(from) ? repeat : exits.get(from).length >= 2 ? choice : single;
+    elements.push(`<path d="M${x(a) + 51} ${y(a)}C${x(a) + 90} ${y(a)} ${x(b) - 90} ${y(b)} ${x(b) - 51} ${y(b)}" fill="none" stroke="${edgeColor}" stroke-width="2"/>`);
+  }
+  for (const stop of stops.values()) {
+    const color = colors[stop.type];
+    elements.push(rect(x(stop) - 51, y(stop) - 14, 102, 28, 7, '#101827', color));
+    elements.push(text(stop.type.toUpperCase(), x(stop), y(stop) - 11, 2, color, 'center'));
+  }
+  return elements.join('\n');
+}
+
+function diagram(seed, base, before, after) {
+  const code = `ROUTE-1176-${String(seed).padStart(2, '0')}`;
+  const elements = [
+    rect(0, 0, 1280, 700, 0, '#0c1423'),
+    text(code, 34, 24, 3, ink),
+    text(`SECTOR ${seed}   |   SEED ${seed}`, 34, 62, 2, faint),
+    text('SECTOR MAP ROUTE CHOICES', 930, 29, 2, faint),
+  ];
+  const headings = ['0 START', '1', '2', '3', '4', '5 REST', '6 BOSS'];
+  for (const [i, label] of headings.entries()) {
+    elements.push(text(label, originX + columnStep * i, 98, 2, ink, 'center'));
+  }
+  elements.push(panel(before, 128, `BEFORE / MAIN ${base.slice(0, 8).toUpperCase()}`));
+  elements.push(panel(after, 350, 'AFTER / ISSUE 1176 WORKING TREE'));
+  for (const [i, [label, color]] of [['SINGLE EXIT', single], ['CHOICE', choice], ['SAME-TYPE FORK', repeat]].entries()) {
+    const x = 34 + i * 234;
+    elements.push(rect(x, 580, 28, 3, 1, color));
+    elements.push(text(label, x + 38, 570, 2, ink));
+  }
+  return `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="1280" height="700">\n${elements.join('\n')}\n</svg>\n`;
+}
+
+const [, , output, ref = 'origin/main'] = process.argv;
+if (!output) throw new Error('Usage: node draw_maps.js <output-directory> [before-revision]');
+const base = git('rev-parse', '--verify', `${ref}^{commit}`).toString().trim();
+const temp = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'voidsworn-route-before-'));
+try {
+  for (const file of [...FILES, 'ui3d.js']) fs.writeFileSync(path.join(temp, file), git('show', `${base}:${file}`));
+  const before = loadMaps(temp);
+  const after = loadMaps(repo);
+  fs.mkdirSync(output, { recursive: true });
+  for (const [index, seed] of examples.entries()) {
+    const name = `route-1176-${String(seed).padStart(2, '0')}.svg`;
+    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(output, name), diagram(seed, base, before[index], after[index]));
+  }
+} finally {
+  for (const file of [...FILES, 'ui3d.js']) fs.rmSync(path.join(temp, file), { force: true });
+  fs.rmdirSync(temp);
+}

 The evidence behind "G3 campaign rules: done, with parity". Every run below can be repeated with the command next to it.

Step 9b is a number change inside the RAMP table only, so it reaches Godot through the re-exported tables with no code change. Everything after that in `godot/campaign/` is the test harness or notes; the screens (`godot/ui/campaign/`) never change campaign state (every button calls `campaign.act()`), so map and panel work cannot move parity.

+extends SceneTree
+
+const CampaignScript = preload("res://campaign/campaign.gd")
+const JSM = preload("res://rules/battle_api.gd").JSM
+const SECTORS := 3000
+const REST_TYPES := ["shipyard", "drydock", "field"]
+
+func _init() -> void:
+	var campaign = CampaignScript.new()
+	var err: String = campaign.load_tables()
+	if not err.is_empty():
+		printerr(err)
+		quit(1)
+		return
+	var eligible := 0
+	var two_exits := 0
+	var same_type := 0
+	var first_repeat := ""
+	for seed_value in range(1, SECTORS + 1):
+		var sector := 1 + (seed_value - 1) % 3
+		JSM.rnd_seed(seed_value)
+		var map: Dictionary = campaign.genSector(sector)
+		var problem := layout_problem(map)
+		if not problem.is_empty():
+			printerr("FAIL: seed %d, sector %d: %s" % [seed_value, sector, problem])
+			quit(1)
+			return
+		var types_by_id := {}
+		for col in map["cols"]:
+			for stop in col: types_by_id[stop["id"]] = stop["type"]
+		var next_types := {}
+		for edge in map["edges"]:
+			if not next_types.has(edge[0]): next_types[edge[0]] = []
+			next_types[edge[0]].append(types_by_id[edge[1]])
+		for col in range(5):
+			for stop in map["cols"][col]:
+				var exits: Array = next_types[stop["id"]]
+				if col > 0:
+					eligible += 1
+					if exits.size() == 2: two_exits += 1
+				if exits.size() == 2 and exits[0] == exits[1]:
+					same_type += 1
+					if first_repeat.is_empty():
+						first_repeat = "seed %d, stop %s, type %s" % [seed_value, stop["id"], exits[0]]
+	var rate := 100.0 * two_exits / eligible
+	print("Sector routes: %d sectors, %d/%d stops in columns 1 to 4 with two exits (%.2f%%), %d same-type forks" % [SECTORS, two_exits, eligible, rate, same_type])
+	if same_type > 0: printerr("First same-type fork: " + first_repeat)
+	var passed := rate >= 80.0 and same_type == 0
+	print("PASS: sector route choices" if passed else "FAIL: expected at least 80% two-exit stops and zero same-type forks")
+	quit(0 if passed else 1)
+
+func layout_problem(map: Dictionary) -> String:
+	var cols: Array = map["cols"]
+	if cols.size() != 7 or cols[0].size() != 1 or cols[6].size() != 1 or cols[6][0]["type"] != "boss":
+		return "expected seven columns with a single start and a single boss at column 6"
+	if cols[5].is_empty() or not cols[5].all(func(stop): return stop["type"] in REST_TYPES):
+		return "expected only rest stops in column 5"
+	if not cols[5].any(func(stop): return stop["type"] in ["shipyard", "drydock"]):
+		return "expected a shipyard or drydock in column 5"
+	var columns := {}
+	for col in cols:
+		for stop in col: columns[stop["id"]] = stop["col"]
+	var incoming := {}
+	var outgoing := {}
+	for edge in map["edges"]:
+		var source: String = edge[0]
+		var target: String = edge[1]
+		if not columns.has(source) or not columns.has(target) or columns[target] != columns[source] + 1:
+			return "edge %s to %s must join consecutive columns" % [source, target]
+		if not outgoing.has(source): outgoing[source] = {}
+		if outgoing[source].has(target): return "duplicate edge %s to %s" % [source, target]
+		outgoing[source][target] = true
+		incoming[target] = true
+	for id in columns:
+		if columns[id] > 0 and not incoming.has(id): return "unreachable stop " + id
+		if columns[id] < 6 and not outgoing.has(id): return "dead end at " + id
+	return ""

+# Sector map route choices
+
+Every stop in columns 1 to 4 offers two routes to different types of stops. The opening stop links to every stop in column 1. Column 5 offers rest stops, and every rest stop leads to the boss in column 6.
+
+The check generated 3,000 sectors with seeds 1 to 3,000, rotating through sectors 1, 2 and 3. It counted stops with exactly two exits in columns 1 to 4 and checked every two-way fork for repeated stop types, including forks at the starting stop.
+
+| Source | Two-exit stops, columns 1 to 4 | Same-type forks |
+|---|---:|---:|
+| Before, main `da81dd82` | 14,778 of 35,905 (41.16%) | 3,463 |
+| After, issue 1176 | 35,905 of 35,905 (100%) | 0 |
+
+The brief cites 58.7% for main. The table reports what this check measured. All stops remained reachable in both samples.
+
+Campaign parity passed for seeds 1 through 30, and the first 40 steps of seed 1 matched in the separate save hand-off check. The [campaign parity record](../../../godot/campaign/test/RECORD.md) has the table checksum and save counts.
+
+## Before and after
+
+Each diagram shows the same seed and sector before and after the change. The labels on the stops give their types. Red lines mark repeated types at a two-way fork. Blue lines mark choices.
+
+![ROUTE-1176-01, sector 1, seed 1](../issue-1176-codex/route-1176-01.png)
+
+![ROUTE-1176-02, sector 2, seed 2](../issue-1176-codex/route-1176-02.png)
+
+![ROUTE-1176-03, sector 3, seed 3](../issue-1176-codex/route-1176-03.png)
+
+Run `node docs/shots/route_choice_1176/draw_maps.js <scratch-directory> da81dd82` from the repo root to redraw the SVG diagrams. To rerun the sector check, use `godot --headless --path godot --script res://campaign/test/sector_routes_test.gd`.

+'use strict';
+
+// node draw_maps.js <output-directory> [before-revision]
+// Draws the route diagrams as SVG. The review copies are rasterized and stamped separately.
+const fs = require('node:fs');
+const os = require('node:os');
+const path = require('node:path');
+const { execFileSync } = require('node:child_process');
+
+const repo = path.resolve(__dirname, '../../..');
+const harness = path.join(repo, 'godot/campaign/test/node/env.js');
+const { FILES } = require(harness);
+const font = require(path.join(repo, 'tools/picture_font.json'));
+const examples = [1, 2, 3];
+const colors = {
+  battle: '#e6ba8c', elite: '#f69082', event: '#b3a7ef',
+  shipyard: '#8fd9c4', drydock: '#82cfe9', field: '#d5db98', boss: '#f7af6b',
+};
+const originX = 112;
+const columnStep = 176;
+const ink = '#edf0f7';
+const faint = '#a8b6cd';
+const single = '#627897';
+const choice = '#79caff';
+const repeat = '#fc9f87';
+
+function git(...args) {
+  return execFileSync('git', ['-C', repo, ...args]);
+}
+
+function loadMaps(oracle) {
+  const sample = `
+    function sampleMap(seed) {
+      rndSeed(seed);
+      return genSector(1 + (seed - 1) % 3);
+    }
+    console.log(JSON.stringify(${JSON.stringify(examples)}.map(sampleMap)));
+  `;
+  const loader = 'require(process.argv[1]).load(process.argv[2])';
+  return JSON.parse(execFileSync(process.execPath, ['-e', loader, harness, sample], {
+    env: { ...process.env, VS_ORACLE: oracle },
+  }));
+}
+
+function text(label, x, y, scale, fill, align = 'left') {
+  // The bundled bitmap font becomes paths, so a CPU SVG rasterizer needs no fonts.
+  if (align === 'center') x -= label.length * 3 * scale;
+  let pixels = '';
+  for (const [index, letter] of [...label].entries()) {
+    const glyph = font[letter] || font['?'];
+    for (const [row, bits] of glyph.entries()) {
+      for (let col = 0; col < 6; col++) {
+        if (bits & (1 << (5 - col))) pixels += `M${index * 6 + col} ${row}h1v1h-1z`;
+      }
+    }
+  }
+  return `<path d="${pixels}" transform="translate(${x} ${y}) scale(${scale})" fill="${fill}"/>`;
+}
+
+function rect(x, y, w, h, radius, fill, stroke = 'none') {
+  return `<rect x="${x}" y="${y}" width="${w}" height="${h}" rx="${radius}" fill="${fill}" stroke="${stroke}"/>`;
+}
+
+function panel(map, top, label) {
+  const stops = new Map(map.cols.flat().map(stop => [stop.id, stop]));
+  const exits = new Map([...stops.keys()].map(id => [id, []]));
+  for (const [from, to] of map.edges) exits.get(from).push(stops.get(to));
+  const eligible = map.cols.slice(1, 5).flat();
+  const two = eligible.filter(stop => exits.get(stop.id).length === 2).length;
+  const sameType = id => {
+    const next = exits.get(id);
+    return next.length === 2 && next[0].type === next[1].type;
+  };
+  const repeated = [...stops.keys()].filter(sameType).length;
+  const y = stop => Math.round(top + 49 + 142 * stop.y);
+  const x = stop => originX + columnStep * stop.col;
+  const elements = [
+    rect(32, top, 1216, 198, 16, '#141f32', '#2a3a55'),
+    text(label, 51, top + 14, 2, ink),
+    text(`${two}/${eligible.length} TWO EXITS IN COLS 1-4   |   SAME-TYPE FORKS: ${repeated}`, 580, top + 14, 2, faint),
+  ];
+  for (const [from, to] of map.edges) {
+    const a = stops.get(from), b = stops.get(to);
+    const edgeColor = sameType(from) ? repeat : exits.get(from).length >= 2 ? choice : single;
+    elements.push(`<path d="M${x(a) + 51} ${y(a)}C${x(a) + 90} ${y(a)} ${x(b) - 90} ${y(b)} ${x(b) - 51} ${y(b)}" fill="none" stroke="${edgeColor}" stroke-width="2"/>`);
+  }
+  for (const stop of stops.values()) {
+    const color = colors[stop.type];
+    elements.push(rect(x(stop) - 51, y(stop) - 14, 102, 28, 7, '#101827', color));
+    elements.push(text(stop.type.toUpperCase(), x(stop), y(stop) - 11, 2, color, 'center'));
+  }
+  return elements.join('\n');
+}
+
+function diagram(seed, base, before, after) {
+  const code = `ROUTE-1176-${String(seed).padStart(2, '0')}`;
+  const elements = [
+    rect(0, 0, 1280, 700, 0, '#0c1423'),
+    text(code, 34, 24, 3, ink),
+    text(`SECTOR ${seed}   |   SEED ${seed}`, 34, 62, 2, faint),
+    text('SECTOR MAP ROUTE CHOICES', 930, 29, 2, faint),
+  ];
+  const headings = ['0 START', '1', '2', '3', '4', '5 REST', '6 BOSS'];
+  for (const [i, label] of headings.entries()) {
+    elements.push(text(label, originX + columnStep * i, 98, 2, ink, 'center'));
+  }
+  elements.push(panel(before, 128, `BEFORE / MAIN ${base.slice(0, 8).toUpperCase()}`));
+  elements.push(panel(after, 350, 'AFTER / ISSUE 1176 WORKING TREE'));
+  for (const [i, [label, color]] of [['SINGLE EXIT', single], ['CHOICE', choice], ['SAME-TYPE FORK', repeat]].entries()) {
+    const x = 34 + i * 234;
+    elements.push(rect(x, 580, 28, 3, 1, color));
+    elements.push(text(label, x + 38, 570, 2, ink));
+  }
+  return `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="1280" height="700">\n${elements.join('\n')}\n</svg>\n`;
+}
+
+const [, , output, ref = 'origin/main'] = process.argv;
+if (!output) throw new Error('Usage: node draw_maps.js <output-directory> [before-revision]');
+const base = git('rev-parse', '--verify', `${ref}^{commit}`).toString().trim();
+const temp = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'voidsworn-route-before-'));
+try {
+  for (const file of [...FILES, 'ui3d.js']) fs.writeFileSync(path.join(temp, file), git('show', `${base}:${file}`));
+  const before = loadMaps(temp);
+  const after = loadMaps(repo);
+  fs.mkdirSync(output, { recursive: true });
+  for (const [index, seed] of examples.entries()) {
+    const name = `route-1176-${String(seed).padStart(2, '0')}.svg`;
+    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(output, name), diagram(seed, base, before[index], after[index]));
+  }
+} finally {
+  for (const file of [...FILES, 'ui3d.js']) fs.rmSync(path.join(temp, file), { force: true });
+  fs.rmdirSync(temp);
+}

 # Campaign test record (G3)
 
 The evidence behind "G3 campaign rules: done, with parity". Every run below can be repeated with the command next to it.
-Newest first. Each row names the commit the run used and where its log lives.
+Newest first. Each row names the source revision the run used and where its evidence lives.
 
 ## What the oracle is
 
-- The game's own JS campaign files, pinned unchanged: `godot/campaign/test/oracle/`, copied from **8e81d75** (Nov 1 2026: stream B step 9b, the RAMP sector 2 budget, the last rules change; before that f770e06, steps 10 to 12, the lore round 8 strings and the campaign battle-start fix; before that d8b47c6, steps 0 to 4, with one local comment fix). See `oracle/README.md`.
-- The campaign rules in Godot last changed at **ae6073b** (port of stream B step 4, oracle d8b47c6). Step 9b is a number change inside the RAMP table only, so it reaches Godot through the re-exported tables with no code change. Everything after that in `godot/campaign/` is the test harness or notes; the screens (`godot/ui/campaign/`) never change campaign state (every button calls `campaign.act()`), so map and panel work cannot move parity.
+- The Node harness reads the game's own JS campaign files at the repo root. The Godot harness uses `campaign.gd`. The older rows below name the pinned oracle each used at the time.
 - The tables (`data/campaign_tables.json`) are exported from the oracle by `node/export_campaign.js`; every parity run first re-exports them and checks the checksum.

+extends SceneTree
+
+const CampaignScript = preload("res://campaign/campaign.gd")
+const JSM = preload("res://rules/battle_api.gd").JSM
+const SECTORS := 3000
+const REST_TYPES := ["shipyard", "drydock", "field"]
+
+func _init() -> void:
+	var campaign = CampaignScript.new()
+	var err: String = campaign.load_tables()
+	if not err.is_empty():
+		printerr(err)
+		quit(1)
+		return
+	var eligible := 0
+	var two_exits := 0
+	var same_type := 0
+	var first_repeat := ""
+	for seed_value in range(1, SECTORS + 1):
+		var sector := 1 + (seed_value - 1) % 3
+		JSM.rnd_seed(seed_value)
+		var map: Dictionary = campaign.genSector(sector)
+		var problem := layout_problem(map)
+		if not problem.is_empty():
+			printerr("FAIL: seed %d, sector %d: %s" % [seed_value, sector, problem])
+			quit(1)
+			return
+		var types_by_id := {}
+		for col in map["cols"]:
+			for stop in col: types_by_id[stop["id"]] = stop["type"]
+		var next_types := {}
+		for edge in map["edges"]:
+			if not next_types.has(edge[0]): next_types[edge[0]] = []
+			next_types[edge[0]].append(types_by_id[edge[1]])
+		for col in range(5):
+			for stop in map["cols"][col]:
+				var exits: Array = next_types[stop["id"]]
+				if col > 0:
+					eligible += 1
+					if exits.size() == 2: two_exits += 1
+				if exits.size() == 2 and exits[0] == exits[1]:
+					same_type += 1
+					if first_repeat.is_empty():
+						first_repeat = "seed %d, stop %s, type %s" % [seed_value, stop["id"], exits[0]]
+	var rate := 100.0 * two_exits / eligible
+	print("Sector routes: %d sectors, %d/%d stops in columns 1 to 4 with two exits (%.2f%%), %d same-type forks" % [SECTORS, two_exits, eligible, rate, same_type])
+	if same_type > 0: printerr("First same-type fork: " + first_repeat)
+	var passed := rate >= 80.0 and same_type == 0
+	print("PASS: sector route choices" if passed else "FAIL: expected at least 80% two-exit stops and zero same-type forks")
+	quit(0 if passed else 1)
+
+func layout_problem(map: Dictionary) -> String:
+	var cols: Array = map["cols"]
+	if cols.size() != 7 or cols[0].size() != 1 or cols[6].size() != 1 or cols[6][0]["type"] != "boss":
+		return "expected seven columns with a single start and a single boss at column 6"
+	if cols[5].is_empty() or not cols[5].all(func(stop): return stop["type"] in REST_TYPES):
+		return "expected only rest stops in column 5"
+	if not cols[5].any(func(stop): return stop["type"] in ["shipyard", "drydock"]):
+		return "expected a shipyard or drydock in column 5"
+	var columns := {}
+	for col in cols:
+		for stop in col: columns[stop["id"]] = stop["col"]
+	var incoming := {}
+	var outgoing := {}
+	for edge in map["edges"]:
+		var source: String = edge[0]
+		var target: String = edge[1]
+		if not columns.has(source) or not columns.has(target) or columns[target] != columns[source] + 1:
+			return "edge %s to %s must join consecutive columns" % [source, target]
+		if not outgoing.has(source): outgoing[source] = {}
+		if outgoing[source].has(target): return "duplicate edge %s to %s" % [source, target]
+		outgoing[source][target] = true
+		incoming[target] = true
+	for id in columns:
+		if columns[id] > 0 and not incoming.has(id): return "unreachable stop " + id
+		if columns[id] < 6 and not outgoing.has(id): return "dead end at " + id
+	return ""

// Issue 946: under --fast a row runs when it is marked fast or it is the gate.
function fastSelected(checks) {
  return checks.filter(c => c.fast || c.gate);
}

// ---------- running ----------

function printRow(row) {
  console.log(row.result + ': ' + row.check + ' (' + row.seconds.toFixed(2) + ' s) | ' + row.detail);
  if (row.result === 'FAIL') console.log('\nFAIL ' + row.check + ' (first 15 output lines):\n' + row.output.split(/\r?\n/).slice(0, 15).join('\n'));
}

async function execute(c, baseline) {
  const start = Date.now();
  const row = { check: c.name, result: 'FAIL', seconds: 0, detail: '', output: '' };
  let temp;
  try {
    const original = path.join(ROOT, c.file);
    const source = fs.readFileSync(original, 'utf8');
    let childArgs = [original, ...c.args];
    const compact = source.trim().replace(/\s/g, '');
    if (compact.length && compact.length % 4 === 0 && /^[A-Za-z0-9+/]+={0,2}$/.test(compact)) {
      const decoded = Buffer.from(compact, 'base64');
      if (decoded.toString('base64') !== compact) throw new Error('Invalid base64');
      temp = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(__dirname, '.checks-temp-'));
      const target = path.join(temp, 'decoded.js');
      fs.writeFileSync(target, decoded);
      // Compile the temporary source with the original module location so sibling
      // requires and __dirname keep working without changing the stored tool.
      const bootstrap = "const fs=require('fs'),M=require('module'),p=require('path');const f=process.argv[1],t=process.argv[2];process.argv=[process.execPath,f,...process.argv.slice(3)];const m=new M(f);m.filename=f;m.paths=M._nodeModulePaths(p.dirname(f));require.main=m;m._compile(fs.readFileSync(t,'utf8'),f);";
      childArgs = ['-e', bootstrap, original, target, ...c.args];
    }
    const run = await new Promise(resolve => {
      const child = spawn(process.execPath, childArgs, { cwd: ROOT, env: { ...process.env, VS_ROOT: ROOT }, windowsHide: true });
      let output = '', error, timedOut = false;
      const timer = setTimeout(() => {
        timedOut = true;
        if (process.platform === 'win32') {
          const killer = spawn('taskkill', ['/pid', String(child.pid), '/T', '/F'], { windowsHide: true, stdio: 'ignore' });
          killer.on('error', () => child.kill());
        } else child.kill('SIGKILL');
      }, c.timeout * 1000);
      child.stdout.on('data', b => { output += b; });
      child.stderr.on('data', b => { output += b; });
      child.on('error', e => { error = e.message; });
      child.on('close', code => { clearTimeout(timer); resolve({ output, code, error, timedOut }); });
    });
    row.output = ascii(run.output);
    row.exitCode = run.code;
    if (run.timedOut) { row.detail = 'FAIL: timed out after ' + c.timeout + ' s'; return row; }
    if (run.error) throw new Error(run.error);
    if (c.kind === 'optional' && run.code !== 0 && /ENOENT|Cannot find module|not recognised|Godot.*(?:not found|required|missing)/i.test(run.output)) {
      row.result = 'SKIP';
      row.detail = 'SKIP: ' + (run.output.split(/\r?\n/).find(l => /ENOENT|Cannot find module|not recognised|Godot/i.test(l)) || 'Missing dependency');
      return row;
    }
    let verdict;
    if (c.kind === 'naming') verdict = classifyNaming(run, baseline);
    else if (c.kind === 'pack') verdict = classifyPack(run, baseline);
    else if (c.kind === 'findings') verdict = classifyFindings(run, baseline);
    else if (c.kind === 'drift') verdict = classifyDrift(run, baseline);
    else verdict = classifyGeneric(run);

async function main() {
  const args = process.argv.slice(2);
  let only;
  for (let i = 0; i < args.length; i++) {
    if (args[i] === '--only' && args[i + 1] && !args[i + 1].startsWith('--')) only = args[++i];
    else if (!['--json', '--fast'].includes(args[i])) { console.error('Usage: node run_all_checks.js [--json] [--fast] [--only <name>]'); process.exit(1); }
  }
  const baseline = readBaseline();
  const checks = buildChecks();
  if (only && !checks.some(c => c.name === only || c.file === only)) { console.error('Unknown check: ' + only + '\nNames: ' + checks.map(c => c.name).join(', ')); process.exit(1); }
  const report = [];
  for (const c of checks.filter(c => !only || c.name === only || c.file === only)) {
    // A gating row is never downgraded to SKIP: --fast still runs it, and a SKIP that still happens is a FAIL.
    // Issue 946: --fast selects by the fast mark from the timed run, not by the limit.
    if (args.includes('--fast') && !c.fast && !c.gate) {
      const row = { check: c.name, result: 'SKIP', seconds: 0, detail: 'SKIP: fast', output: '' };
      report.push(row);
      console.log('Running ' + c.name + ' (limit ' + c.timeout + ' s)...');
      printRow(row);
      continue;
    }
    console.log('Running ' + c.name + ' (limit ' + c.timeout + ' s)...');
    const row = await execute(c, baseline);
    if (c.gate && row.result === 'SKIP') { row.result = 'FAIL'; row.detail = 'FAIL: gating row would skip: ' + row.detail; }
    report.push(row);
    printRow(row);
  }
  console.log('\ncheck | result | seconds | detail');
  console.log('--- | --- | --- | ---');
  for (const r of report) console.log(`${r.check} | ${r.result} | ${r.seconds.toFixed(2)} | ${r.detail}`);
  const skipped = report.filter(r => r.result === 'SKIP').map(r => r.check);
  if (skipped.length) console.log('\nSKIP: ' + skipped.join(', '));
  if (args.includes('--json')) fs.writeFileSync(path.join(ROOT, 'checks_report.json'), JSON.stringify(report, null, 2) + '\n');
  process.exitCode = exitCodeFor(report);
}

if (require.main === module) {
  main().catch(e => { console.error(ascii(e.stack)); process.exitCode = 1; });

func _run() -> void:
	scratch = OS.get_environment("TEMP") if OS.has_environment("TEMP") else "/tmp"
	scratch = scratch.path_join("voidsworn_resume_%d_%d" % [OS.get_process_id(), Time.get_ticks_usec()])
	if DirAccess.make_dir_absolute(scratch) != OK:
		printerr("FAIL: could not create isolated save folder")
		quit(2)
		return
	_story_boss()
	await _breakthrough()
	_backup_continue()
	_boss_payout()
	_prize_reload()
	_yard_doctrine()
	_career_backup()
	quit(1 if failures else 0)

func _check(ok: bool, message: String) -> void:
	print(("PASS: " if ok else "FAIL: ") + message)
	if not ok: failures += 1

func _campaign(sector := 1):
	var c := SaveProbe.new()
	assert(c.load_tables() == "")
	API.JSM.rnd_seed(1091)
	c.fixed_clock = true
	c.newRun("vex", "voidsworn")
	if sector > 1:
		c.RUN["sector"] = sector
		c.RUN["map"] = c.genSector(sector)
		c.saveRun()
	c.mapScreen()
	return c

func _load(text: String):
	var c = _campaign()
	c.titleScreen()
	c.load_save_text(text)
	return c

func _legacy(name_: String) -> String:
	var text := FileAccess.get_file_as_string(LEGACY + name_ + ".json")
	assert(not text.is_empty(), "Missing old save: " + name_)
	return text

func _external(c):
	var api = API.new()
	assert(api.load_rules() == "")
	c.battle_mode = "external"
	c.battle_setup = func(run, cfg):
		api.new_battle(run, cfg)
		return api.R.B
	return api

# Reach the column before the real boss without simulating the preceding fights.
func _boss_approach(c) -> String:
	var cols: Array = c.RUN["map"]["cols"]
	var id: String = cols.back()[0]["id"]
	var edge: Array = c.RUN["map"]["edges"].filter(func(e): return e[1] == id)[0]
	c.RUN["pos"] = edge[0]
		if sector == 3:
			c.showBeat3(); c.act("eventChoice", "0"); c.act("closeModal")
		c.act("enterNode", id)
		assert(c.page["kind"] == "battle")
		c.act("battle", "win")
		assert(c.RUN["reward"]["kind"] == "boss")
		var legacy := ""
		var before_credits: float = c.RUN["credits"]
		for i in range(20):
			if c.RUN == null or c.RUN.get("reward") == null: break
			var acts: Array = c.screen_acts().filter(func(a): return a[2] == 1)
			var click: Array = acts[0]
			c.writes.clear()
			c.act(click[0], click[1])
			for text in c.writes:
				var run = JSON.parse_string(text)["run"]
				if run.get("pos") == id and run.get("sector") == sector and not run.has("reward"): legacy = text
		_check(legacy == "" and (c.page["kind"] == "end" if sector == 3 else c.RUN["sector"] == 2),
			"5: finishing sector %d never saves a paid boss with no onward route" % sector)
		var d = _load(_legacy("boss_%d" % sector))
		var credits_once: float = d.SAVED_RUN["credits"]

		d.act("continueRun")
		var advanced: bool = d.page["kind"] == "end" and d.SAVED_RUN == null if sector == 3 else d.RUN["sector"] == 2 and d.RUN["cpCap"] == cap_once + 3 and not d.availableNodes().is_empty()
		var settled: Dictionary = d.page["data"]["run"] if d.page["kind"] == "end" else d.RUN
		_check(advanced and settled["credits"] == credits_once and credits_once >= before_credits,
			"5 legacy: paid sector %d boss advances on Continue without paying again" % sector)

func _prize_reload() -> void:
	var c = _campaign()
	var id = c.availableNodes()[0]
	c.RUN["curNode"] = id
	c.RUN["pos"] = id
	var api = _external(c)
	c.battleScreen({"kind": "elite", "obj": "rout", "fac": "hegemony", "col": 1, "node": id,
		"seed": 1091, "map": "belt", "shape": "open", "enemies": [{"tpl": "h_picket"}, {"tpl": "h_picket"}]})
	var r = api.R
	var foes: Array = r.caps("enemy")
	foes[0]["side"] = "player"; foes[0]["captured"] = true
	foes[1]["hp"] = 0
	r.killUnit(foes[1], r.caps("player")[0])
	c.finish_battle(r.B["over"], r.B)
	c.act("prizeStep"); c.act("prizeSet", "0:ransom")
	c.writes.clear()
	c.act("prizeConfirm")
	var credits: float = c.RUN["credits"]
	var old: Dictionary = JSON.parse_string(c.writes[0])
	_check(old["run"]["reward"]["stage"] == "salvage", "7: the first paid-prize save already points to wreck salvage")
	var d = _load(c.writes[0])
	d.act("continueRun")
	_check(_salvage_then_reward(d, credits), "7: reloading a paid prize shows salvage and then the next reward, with one ransom")
	d = _load(_legacy("prize"))
	d.act("continueRun")
	_check(_salvage_then_reward(d, credits), "7 legacy: an empty prize stage still shows salvage and the next reward")

func _salvage_then_reward(c, credits: float) -> bool:
	if c.modal_d == null or c.modal_d["kind"] != "salvage" or c.RUN["credits"] != credits or c.RUN["ransoms"] != 1: return false
	c.act("takeSalvage", "0:cred")
	return c.modal_d != null and c.modal_d["kind"] in ["boon", "mod", "relic"] and c.RUN["credits"] > credits and c.RUN["ransoms"] == 1

func _yard_doctrine() -> void:
	var c = _campaign(2)
	var yard = c.availableNodes()[0]
	c.act("enterNode", yard)
	c.act("yardFleet")
	assert(c.page["kind"] == "fleet" and c.verbCanUse())
	c.act("factionVerb")
	var d = _load(c.save_text())
	d.act("continueRun")
	_check(d.page["kind"] == "shipyard" and d.page["data"]["node"]["id"] == yard and not d.verbCanUse(),
		"8: reloading after a yard doctrine purchase returns to the same yard")
	if c.page["kind"] == "fleet" and c.page["data"].get("back") == "shipyard": c.act("backShipyard")
	var still_in_yard: bool = c.page["kind"] == "shipyard" and not c.findNode(yard).get("done", false)
	if still_in_yard: c.act("leaveYard")
	_check(still_in_yard and c.page["kind"] == "map" and c.findNode(yard).get("done", false) and not c.verbCanUse(),
		"8: buying doctrine from the yard fleet leaves the same yard open until Leave")
	c = _campaign(2)
	c.act("fleetScreen"); c.act("factionVerb")
	if c.page["kind"] == "fleet" and c.page["data"].get("back") == "map": c.act("backMap")
	_check(c.page["kind"] == "map" and not c.verbCanUse(), "8: buying the same doctrine from the map fleet still returns to the map")

	c.save_profile()

	c.save_profile()
	var d := Career.new()
	d.save_path = c.save_path

# stable merge sort with a JS-style comparator (negative, zero or positive)
static func ssort(a: Array, cmp: Callable) -> Array:
	var n := a.size()
	if n < 2: return a
	var src := a.duplicate(); var dst := src.duplicate(); var width := 1
	while width < n:
		var i := 0
		while i < n:
			var mid := mini(i + width, n); var end := mini(i + 2 * width, n); var l := i; var r := mid; var k := i
			while l < mid and r < end:
				if float(cmp.call(src[r], src[l])) < 0.0:
					dst[k] = src[r]; r += 1
				else:
					dst[k] = src[l]; l += 1
				k += 1
			while l < mid:
				dst[k] = src[l]; l += 1; k += 1
			while r < end:
				dst[k] = src[r]; r += 1; k += 1
			i += 2 * width
		var tmp := src; src = dst; dst = tmp; width *= 2
	for i in range(n): a[i] = src[i]
	return a
static func has_same(a: Array, x) -> bool: # Array.prototype.includes for objects (identity)
	for v in a:
		if typeof(v) == typeof(x) and is_same(v, x): return true
	return false
static func index_same(a: Array, x) -> int:
	for i in range(a.size()):
		if typeof(a[i]) == typeof(x) and is_same(a[i], x): return i
	return -1

+# Sector map route choices
+
+Every stop in columns 1 to 4 offers two routes to different types of stops. The opening stop links to every stop in column 1. Column 5 offers rest stops, and every rest stop leads to the boss in column 6.
+
+The check generated 3,000 sectors with seeds 1 to 3,000, rotating through sectors 1, 2 and 3. It counted stops with exactly two exits in columns 1 to 4 and checked every two-way fork for repeated stop types, including forks at the starting stop.
+
+| Source | Two-exit stops, columns 1 to 4 | Same-type forks |
+|---|---:|---:|
+| Before, main `da81dd82` | 14,778 of 35,905 (41.16%) | 3,463 |
+| After, issue 1176 | 35,905 of 35,905 (100%) | 0 |
+
+The brief cites 58.7% for main. The table reports what this check measured. All stops remained reachable in both samples.
+
+Campaign parity passed for seeds 1 through 30, and the first 40 steps of seed 1 matched in the separate save hand-off check. The [campaign parity record](../../../godot/campaign/test/RECORD.md) has the table checksum and save counts.
+
+## Before and after
+
+Each diagram shows the same seed and sector before and after the change. The labels on the stops give their types. Red lines mark repeated types at a two-way fork. Blue lines mark choices.
+
+![ROUTE-1176-01, sector 1, seed 1](../issue-1176-codex/route-1176-01.png)
+
+![ROUTE-1176-02, sector 2, seed 2](../issue-1176-codex/route-1176-02.png)
+
+![ROUTE-1176-03, sector 3, seed 3](../issue-1176-codex/route-1176-03.png)
+
+Run `node docs/shots/route_choice_1176/draw_maps.js <scratch-directory> da81dd82` from the repo root to redraw the SVG diagrams. To rerun the sector check, use `godot --headless --path godot --script res://campaign/test/sector_routes_test.gd`.

+'use strict';
+
+// node draw_maps.js <output-directory> [before-revision]
+// Draws the route diagrams as SVG. The review copies are rasterized and stamped separately.
+const fs = require('node:fs');
+const os = require('node:os');
+const path = require('node:path');
+const { execFileSync } = require('node:child_process');
+
+const repo = path.resolve(__dirname, '../../..');
+const harness = path.join(repo, 'godot/campaign/test/node/env.js');
+const { FILES } = require(harness);
+const font = require(path.join(repo, 'tools/picture_font.json'));
+const examples = [1, 2, 3];
+const colors = {
+  battle: '#e6ba8c', elite: '#f69082', event: '#b3a7ef',
+  shipyard: '#8fd9c4', drydock: '#82cfe9', field: '#d5db98', boss: '#f7af6b',
+};
+const originX = 112;
+const columnStep = 176;
+const ink = '#edf0f7';
+const faint = '#a8b6cd';
+const single = '#627897';
+const choice = '#79caff';
+const repeat = '#fc9f87';
+
+function git(...args) {
+  return execFileSync('git', ['-C', repo, ...args]);
+}
+
+function loadMaps(oracle) {
+  const sample = `
+    function sampleMap(seed) {
+      rndSeed(seed);
+      return genSector(1 + (seed - 1) % 3);
+    }
+    console.log(JSON.stringify(${JSON.stringify(examples)}.map(sampleMap)));
+  `;
+  const loader = 'require(process.argv[1]).load(process.argv[2])';
+  return JSON.parse(execFileSync(process.execPath, ['-e', loader, harness, sample], {
+    env: { ...process.env, VS_ORACLE: oracle },
+  }));
+}
+
+function text(label, x, y, scale, fill, align = 'left') {
+  // The bundled bitmap font becomes paths, so a CPU SVG rasterizer needs no fonts.
+  if (align === 'center') x -= label.length * 3 * scale;
+  let pixels = '';
+  for (const [index, letter] of [...label].entries()) {
+    const glyph = font[letter] || font['?'];
+    for (const [row, bits] of glyph.entries()) {
+      for (let col = 0; col < 6; col++) {
+        if (bits & (1 << (5 - col))) pixels += `M${index * 6 + col} ${row}h1v1h-1z`;
+      }
+    }
+  }
+  return `<path d="${pixels}" transform="translate(${x} ${y}) scale(${scale})" fill="${fill}"/>`;
+}
+
+function rect(x, y, w, h, radius, fill, stroke = 'none') {
+  return `<rect x="${x}" y="${y}" width="${w}" height="${h}" rx="${radius}" fill="${fill}" stroke="${stroke}"/>`;
+}
+
+function panel(map, top, label) {
+  const stops = new Map(map.cols.flat().map(stop => [stop.id, stop]));
+  const exits = new Map([...stops.keys()].map(id => [id, []]));
+  for (const [from, to] of map.edges) exits.get(from).push(stops.get(to));
+  const eligible = map.cols.slice(1, 5).flat();
+  const two = eligible.filter(stop => exits.get(stop.id).length === 2).length;
+  const sameType = id => {
+    const next = exits.get(id);
+    return next.length === 2 && next[0].type === next[1].type;
+  };
+  const repeated = [...stops.keys()].filter(sameType).length;
+  const y = stop => Math.round(top + 49 + 142 * stop.y);
+  const x = stop => originX + columnStep * stop.col;
+  const elements = [
+    rect(32, top, 1216, 198, 16, '#141f32', '#2a3a55'),
+    text(label, 51, top + 14, 2, ink),
+    text(`${two}/${eligible.length} TWO EXITS IN COLS 1-4   |   SAME-TYPE FORKS: ${repeated}`, 580, top + 14, 2, faint),
+  ];
+  for (const [from, to] of map.edges) {
+    const a = stops.get(from), b = stops.get(to);
+    const edgeColor = sameType(from) ? repeat : exits.get(from).length >= 2 ? choice : single;
+    elements.push(`<path d="M${x(a) + 51} ${y(a)}C${x(a) + 90} ${y(a)} ${x(b) - 90} ${y(b)} ${x(b) - 51} ${y(b)}" fill="none" stroke="${edgeColor}" stroke-width="2"/>`);
+  }
+  for (const stop of stops.values()) {
+    const color = colors[stop.type];
+    elements.push(rect(x(stop) - 51, y(stop) - 14, 102, 28, 7, '#101827', color));
+    elements.push(text(stop.type.toUpperCase(), x(stop), y(stop) - 11, 2, color, 'center'));
+  }
+  return elements.join('\n');
+}
+
+function diagram(seed, base, before, after) {
+  const code = `ROUTE-1176-${String(seed).padStart(2, '0')}`;
+  const elements = [
+    rect(0, 0, 1280, 700, 0, '#0c1423'),
+    text(code, 34, 24, 3, ink),
+    text(`SECTOR ${seed}   |   SEED ${seed}`, 34, 62, 2, faint),
+    text('SECTOR MAP ROUTE CHOICES', 930, 29, 2, faint),
+  ];
+  const headings = ['0 START', '1', '2', '3', '4', '5 REST', '6 BOSS'];
+  for (const [i, label] of headings.entries()) {
+    elements.push(text(label, originX + columnStep * i, 98, 2, ink, 'center'));
+  }
+  elements.push(panel(before, 128, `BEFORE / MAIN ${base.slice(0, 8).toUpperCase()}`));
+  elements.push(panel(after, 350, 'AFTER / ISSUE 1176 WORKING TREE'));
+  for (const [i, [label, color]] of [['SINGLE EXIT', single], ['CHOICE', choice], ['SAME-TYPE FORK', repeat]].entries()) {
+    const x = 34 + i * 234;
+    elements.push(rect(x, 580, 28, 3, 1, color));
+    elements.push(text(label, x + 38, 570, 2, ink));
+  }
+  return `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="1280" height="700">\n${elements.join('\n')}\n</svg>\n`;
+}
+
+const [, , output, ref = 'origin/main'] = process.argv;
+if (!output) throw new Error('Usage: node draw_maps.js <output-directory> [before-revision]');
+const base = git('rev-parse', '--verify', `${ref}^{commit}`).toString().trim();
+const temp = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'voidsworn-route-before-'));
+try {
+  for (const file of [...FILES, 'ui3d.js']) fs.writeFileSync(path.join(temp, file), git('show', `${base}:${file}`));
+  const before = loadMaps(temp);
+  const after = loadMaps(repo);
+  fs.mkdirSync(output, { recursive: true });
+  for (const [index, seed] of examples.entries()) {
+    const name = `route-1176-${String(seed).padStart(2, '0')}.svg`;
+    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(output, name), diagram(seed, base, before[index], after[index]));
+  }
+} finally {
+  for (const file of [...FILES, 'ui3d.js']) fs.rmSync(path.join(temp, file), { force: true });
+  fs.rmdirSync(temp);
+}

 # Campaign test record (G3)
 
 The evidence behind "G3 campaign rules: done, with parity". Every run below can be repeated with the command next to it.
-Newest first. Each row names the commit the run used and where its log lives.
+Newest first. Each row names the source revision the run used and where its evidence lives.
 
 ## What the oracle is
 
-- The game's own JS campaign files, pinned unchanged: `godot/campaign/test/oracle/`, copied from **8e81d75** (Nov 1 2026: stream B step 9b, the RAMP sector 2 budget, the last rules change; before that f770e06, steps 10 to 12, the lore round 8 strings and the campaign battle-start fix; before that d8b47c6, steps 0 to 4, with one local comment fix). See `oracle/README.md`.
-- The campaign rules in Godot last changed at **ae6073b** (port of stream B step 4, oracle d8b47c6). Step 9b is a number change inside the RAMP table only, so it reaches Godot through the re-exported tables with no code change. Everything after that in `godot/campaign/` is the test harness or notes; the screens (`godot/ui/campaign/`) never change campaign state (every button calls `campaign.act()`), so map and panel work cannot move parity.
+- The Node harness reads the game's own JS campaign files at the repo root. The Godot harness uses `campaign.gd`. The older rows below name the pinned oracle each used at the time.
 - The tables (`data/campaign_tables.json`) are exported from the oracle by `node/export_campaign.js`; every parity run first re-exports them and checks the checksum.

+extends SceneTree
+
+const CampaignScript = preload("res://campaign/campaign.gd")
+const JSM = preload("res://rules/battle_api.gd").JSM
+const SECTORS := 3000
+const REST_TYPES := ["shipyard", "drydock", "field"]
+
+func _init() -> void:
+	var campaign = CampaignScript.new()
+	var err: String = campaign.load_tables()
+	if not err.is_empty():
+		printerr(err)
+		quit(1)
+		return
+	var eligible := 0
+	var two_exits := 0
+	var same_type := 0
+	var first_repeat := ""
+	for seed_value in range(1, SECTORS + 1):
+		var sector := 1 + (seed_value - 1) % 3
+		JSM.rnd_seed(seed_value)
+		var map: Dictionary = campaign.genSector(sector)
+		var problem := layout_problem(map)
+		if not problem.is_empty():
+			printerr("FAIL: seed %d, sector %d: %s" % [seed_value, sector, problem])
+			quit(1)
+			return
+		var types_by_id := {}
+		for col in map["cols"]:
+			for stop in col: types_by_id[stop["id"]] = stop["type"]
+		var next_types := {}
+		for edge in map["edges"]:
+			if not next_types.has(edge[0]): next_types[edge[0]] = []
+			next_types[edge[0]].append(types_by_id[edge[1]])
+		for col in range(5):
+			for stop in map["cols"][col]:
+				var exits: Array = next_types[stop["id"]]
+				if col > 0:
+					eligible += 1
+					if exits.size() == 2: two_exits += 1
+				if exits.size() == 2 and exits[0] == exits[1]:
+					same_type += 1
+					if first_repeat.is_empty():
+						first_repeat = "seed %d, stop %s, type %s" % [seed_value, stop["id"], exits[0]]
+	var rate := 100.0 * two_exits / eligible
+	print("Sector routes: %d sectors, %d/%d stops in columns 1 to 4 with two exits (%.2f%%), %d same-type forks" % [SECTORS, two_exits, eligible, rate, same_type])
+	if same_type > 0: printerr("First same-type fork: " + first_repeat)
+	var passed := rate >= 80.0 and same_type == 0
+	print("PASS: sector route choices" if passed else "FAIL: expected at least 80% two-exit stops and zero same-type forks")
+	quit(0 if passed else 1)
+
+func layout_problem(map: Dictionary) -> String:
+	var cols: Array = map["cols"]
+	if cols.size() != 7 or cols[0].size() != 1 or cols[6].size() != 1 or cols[6][0]["type"] != "boss":
+		return "expected seven columns with a single start and a single boss at column 6"
+	if cols[5].is_empty() or not cols[5].all(func(stop): return stop["type"] in REST_TYPES):
+		return "expected only rest stops in column 5"
+	if not cols[5].any(func(stop): return stop["type"] in ["shipyard", "drydock"]):
+		return "expected a shipyard or drydock in column 5"
+	var columns := {}
+	for col in cols:
+		for stop in col: columns[stop["id"]] = stop["col"]
+	var incoming := {}
+	var outgoing := {}
+	for edge in map["edges"]:
+		var source: String = edge[0]
+		var target: String = edge[1]
+		if not columns.has(source) or not columns.has(target) or columns[target] != columns[source] + 1:
+			return "edge %s to %s must join consecutive columns" % [source, target]
+		if not outgoing.has(source): outgoing[source] = {}
+		if outgoing[source].has(target): return "duplicate edge %s to %s" % [source, target]
+		outgoing[source][target] = true
+		incoming[target] = true
+	for id in columns:
+		if columns[id] > 0 and not incoming.has(id): return "unreachable stop " + id
+		if columns[id] < 6 and not outgoing.has(id): return "dead end at " + id
+	return ""

func _all_old(off: Array, old: Array) -> bool:

		else:
			var f = String(pick(C["first"])); n = f + " " + String(pick(C["last"]))
		if not used.has(n.split(" ")[0]): break
	return n

	return n

		show_modal("relic", {"boss": or_(W.get("rewardCfg"), {}).get("kind") == "boss", "opts": rc}, acts); return
	promotionsStep()
func _add_relic(r) -> void: RUN["relics"].append(r)
func ACT_takeRelic(r: String) -> void:
	if not or_(W.get("relicChoice"), []).has(r): return
# Reach the column before the real boss without simulating the preceding fights.
func _boss_approach(c) -> String:
	var cols: Array = c.RUN["map"]["cols"]
	var id: String = cols.back()[0]["id"]
	var edge: Array = c.RUN["map"]["edges"].filter(func(e): return e[1] == id)[0]
	c.RUN["pos"] = edge[0]
	c.findNode(edge[0])["done"] = true
	c.saveRun()
	c.mapScreen()
	return id

func _story_boss() -> void:
	var c = _campaign(3)
	c.save_path = scratch.path_join("boosted.json")
	c.persist_on = true
	var id = _boss_approach(c)
	c.showBeat3()
	c.act("eventChoice", "0")
	c.act("closeModal")
	_external(c)
	c.act("enterNode", id)
	var cfg: Dictionary = c.B["cfg"]
	assert(cfg["storyMods"]["power"])
	var d = _load(FileAccess.get_file_as_string(c.save_path))
	_external(d)
	d.act("continueRun")
	if d.page["kind"] == "map" and d.availableNodes().has(id): d.act("enterNode", id)
	var reopened: bool = d.B != null and d.B["cfg"].get("node") == id and d.B["cfg"].get("seed") == cfg["seed"] and d.B["cfg"].get("storyMods", {}).get("power", false)
	_check(reopened, "1: closing mid-fight lets Continue replay the same boosted boss")
	d = _load(_legacy("story_boss"))
	_external(d)
static func js_int(x: float) -> int: # ToInt32 of a double (x|0)
	if is_nan(x) or is_inf(x): return 0
	var t := int(x) # truncates toward zero; doubles here are far below 2^63
	return to_i32(t)

# ---- PRNG: core.js rndSeed / rnd (mulberry32) ----
static var rng_s: int = 0 # kept as JS keeps it: rndSeed stores s>>>0, rnd stores a signed 32-bit value
static func rnd_seed(s: int) -> void:
	rng_s = to_u32(s)
static func rnd() -> float:
	rng_s = to_i32(rng_s + 0x6D2B79F5)
	var a := to_u32(rng_s)
	var t := imul(a ^ (a >> 15), 1 | a)
	t = to_u32(t + imul(t ^ (t >> 7), 61 | t)) ^ t
	return float(to_u32(t ^ (t >> 14))) / 4294967296.0
static func rng_u32() -> int:
	return to_u32(rng_s)

# ---- Math.round (V8: ceil, then step back if that went more than half up) ----
static func jsround(x: float) -> float:
	if is_nan(x) or is_inf(x): return x
	var c := ceil(x)
	return c if c - 0.5 <= x else c - 1.0

#!/bin/sh
# Runs the headless Godot checks named in godot/tools/ci/godot_checks.txt (issue 359 part b). GitHub runs it; so can any
# Linux checkout with Godot 4.7.2:   GODOT=/path/to/godot sh godot/tools/ci/run_checks.sh [name ...]
# First it proves the list is whole: every .gd file under godot/**/test/ is listed once, and every listed file exists.
# Then each headless line (except the ones another named step runs) runs with a time limit. A check fails on a non-zero
# exit, on the time limit, or on any SCRIPT ERROR line in its log. Prints one line per check and exits 1 on any failure.
# The whole list also has a time budget (GODOT_CHECKS_BUDGET, 780 s; a healthy run takes about 200 s): a check gets
# 300 s or what is left of the budget, whichever is less, and a check the budget never reached is a FAIL, never a quiet
# skip. So this script always ends well inside GitHub's 30 minute job limit, and a red job stays red instead of being
# cancelled (a script that does not compile makes several checks sit until their limit: commit 138ff38, 1020 s).
cd "$(dirname "$0")/../.." || exit 1
P=$(pwd)            # godot/
ROOT=$(cd .. && pwd)
G="${GODOT:-godot}"
LIST="$P/tools/ci/godot_checks.txt"
T="${RUNNER_TEMP:-/tmp}/godot_checks"
rm -rf "$T"; mkdir -p "$T/logs" "$T/fixtures" "$T/xdg"
F=0
BUDGET="${GODOT_CHECKS_BUDGET:-780}"

# 1. the list is whole
grep -v '^#' "$LIST" | grep -v '^$' | cut -d'|' -f2 | sort > "$T/listed.txt"
find . \( -path ./.godot -o -path ./gen \) -prune -o -path '*/test/*' -name '*.gd' -print | sed 's|^\./||' | sort > "$T/found.txt"
dup=$(uniq -d "$T/listed.txt")
miss=$(comm -13 "$T/listed.txt" "$T/found.txt")
gone=$(comm -23 "$T/listed.txt" "$T/found.txt")
bad=$(grep -v '^#' "$LIST" | grep -v '^$' | awk -F'|' 'NF != 5 || ($1 != "headless" && $1 != "gpu" && $1 != "retired") {print}')
if [ -n "$dup$miss$gone$bad" ]; then
  [ -n "$miss" ] && echo "  list: FAIL, test scripts not in godot_checks.txt:" && echo "$miss" | sed 's/^/    /'
  [ -n "$gone" ] && echo "  list: FAIL, listed but missing:" && echo "$gone" | sed 's/^/    /'
  [ -n "$dup" ] && echo "  list: FAIL, listed twice:" && echo "$dup" | sed 's/^/    /'
  [ -n "$bad" ] && echo "  list: FAIL, lines that are not class|script|setup|args|reason:" && echo "$bad" | sed 's/^/    /'
  F=$((F+1))
else
  echo "  list: PASS ($(wc -l < "$T/found.txt") test scripts: $(grep -c '^headless|' "$LIST") headless, $(grep -c '^gpu|' "$LIST") gpu, $(grep -c '^retired|' "$LIST") retired)"
fi

# 2. the headless checks
ran=0; t_all=$(date +%s)
grep '^headless|' "$LIST" > "$T/headless.txt"
while IFS='|' read -r cls script setup args reason; do

apply patch

+# Sector map route choices
+
+Every stop in columns 1 to 4 offers two routes to different types of stops. The opening stop links to every stop in column 1. Column 5 offers rest stops, and every rest stop leads to the boss in column 6.
+
+The check generated 3,000 sectors with seeds 1 to 3,000, rotating through sectors 1, 2 and 3. It counted stops with exactly two exits in columns 1 to 4 and checked every two-way fork for repeated stop types, including forks at the starting stop.
+
+| Source | Two-exit stops, columns 1 to 4 | Same-type forks |
+|---|---:|---:|
+| Before, main `da81dd82` | 14,778 of 35,905 (41.16%) | 3,463 |
+| After, issue 1176 | 35,905 of 35,905 (100%) | 0 |
+
+The brief cites 58.7% for main. The table reports what this check measured. All stops remained reachable in both samples.
+
+Campaign parity passed for seeds 1 through 30, and the first 40 steps of seed 1 matched in the separate save hand-off check. The [campaign parity record](../../../godot/campaign/test/RECORD.md) has the table checksum and save counts.
+
+## Before and after
+
+Each diagram shows the same seed and sector before and after the change. The labels on the stops give their types. Red lines mark repeated types at a two-way fork. Blue lines mark choices.
+
+![ROUTE-1176-01, sector 1, seed 1](../issue-1176-codex/route-1176-01.png)
+
+![ROUTE-1176-02, sector 2, seed 2](../issue-1176-codex/route-1176-02.png)
+
+![ROUTE-1176-03, sector 3, seed 3](../issue-1176-codex/route-1176-03.png)
+
+Run `node docs/shots/route_choice_1176/draw_maps.js <scratch-directory> da81dd82` from the repo root to redraw the SVG diagrams. To rerun the sector check, use `godot --headless --path godot --script res://campaign/test/sector_routes_test.gd`.

+'use strict';
+
+// node draw_maps.js <output-directory> [before-revision]
+// Draws the route diagrams as SVG. The review copies are rasterized and stamped separately.
+const fs = require('node:fs');
+const os = require('node:os');
+const path = require('node:path');
+const { execFileSync } = require('node:child_process');
+
+const repo = path.resolve(__dirname, '../../..');
+const harness = path.join(repo, 'godot/campaign/test/node/env.js');
+const { FILES } = require(harness);
+const font = require(path.join(repo, 'tools/picture_font.json'));
+const examples = [1, 2, 3];
+const colors = {
+  battle: '#e6ba8c', elite: '#f69082', event: '#b3a7ef',
+  shipyard: '#8fd9c4', drydock: '#82cfe9', field: '#d5db98', boss: '#f7af6b',
+};
+const originX = 112;
+const columnStep = 176;
+const ink = '#edf0f7';
+const faint = '#a8b6cd';
+const single = '#627897';
+const choice = '#79caff';
+const repeat = '#fc9f87';
+
+function git(...args) {
+  return execFileSync('git', ['-C', repo, ...args]);
+}
+
+function loadMaps(oracle) {
+  const sample = `
+    function sampleMap(seed) {
+      rndSeed(seed);
+      return genSector(1 + (seed - 1) % 3);
+    }
+    console.log(JSON.stringify(${JSON.stringify(examples)}.map(sampleMap)));
+  `;
+  const loader = 'require(process.argv[1]).load(process.argv[2])';
+  return JSON.parse(execFileSync(process.execPath, ['-e', loader, harness, sample], {
+    env: { ...process.env, VS_ORACLE: oracle },
+  }));
+}
+
+function text(label, x, y, scale, fill, align = 'left') {
+  // The bundled bitmap font becomes paths, so a CPU SVG rasterizer needs no fonts.
+  if (align === 'center') x -= label.length * 3 * scale;
+  let pixels = '';
+  for (const [index, letter] of [...label].entries()) {
+    const glyph = font[letter] || font['?'];
+    for (const [row, bits] of glyph.entries()) {
+      for (let col = 0; col < 6; col++) {
+        if (bits & (1 << (5 - col))) pixels += `M${index * 6 + col} ${row}h1v1h-1z`;
+      }
+    }
+  }
+  return `<path d="${pixels}" transform="translate(${x} ${y}) scale(${scale})" fill="${fill}"/>`;
+}
+
+function rect(x, y, w, h, radius, fill, stroke = 'none') {
+  return `<rect x="${x}" y="${y}" width="${w}" height="${h}" rx="${radius}" fill="${fill}" stroke="${stroke}"/>`;
+}
+
+function panel(map, top, label) {
+  const stops = new Map(map.cols.flat().map(stop => [stop.id, stop]));
+  const exits = new Map([...stops.keys()].map(id => [id, []]));
+  for (const [from, to] of map.edges) exits.get(from).push(stops.get(to));
+  const eligible = map.cols.slice(1, 5).flat();
+  const two = eligible.filter(stop => exits.get(stop.id).length === 2).length;
+  const sameType = id => {
+    const next = exits.get(id);
+    return next.length === 2 && next[0].type === next[1].type;
+  };
+  const repeated = [...stops.keys()].filter(sameType).length;
+  const y = stop => Math.round(top + 49 + 142 * stop.y);
+  const x = stop => originX + columnStep * stop.col;
+  const elements = [
+    rect(32, top, 1216, 198, 16, '#141f32', '#2a3a55'),
+    text(label, 51, top + 14, 2, ink),
+    text(`${two}/${eligible.length} TWO EXITS IN COLS 1-4   |   SAME-TYPE FORKS: ${repeated}`, 580, top + 14, 2, faint),
+  ];
+  for (const [from, to] of map.edges) {
+    const a = stops.get(from), b = stops.get(to);
+    const edgeColor = sameType(from) ? repeat : exits.get(from).length >= 2 ? choice : single;
+    elements.push(`<path d="M${x(a) + 51} ${y(a)}C${x(a) + 90} ${y(a)} ${x(b) - 90} ${y(b)} ${x(b) - 51} ${y(b)}" fill="none" stroke="${edgeColor}" stroke-width="2"/>`);
+  }
+  for (const stop of stops.values()) {
+    const color = colors[stop.type];
+    elements.push(rect(x(stop) - 51, y(stop) - 14, 102, 28, 7, '#101827', color));
+    elements.push(text(stop.type.toUpperCase(), x(stop), y(stop) - 11, 2, color, 'center'));
+  }
+  return elements.join('\n');
+}
+
+function diagram(seed, base, before, after) {
+  const code = `ROUTE-1176-${String(seed).padStart(2, '0')}`;
+  const elements = [
+    rect(0, 0, 1280, 700, 0, '#0c1423'),
+    text(code, 34, 24, 3, ink),
+    text(`SECTOR ${seed}   |   SEED ${seed}`, 34, 62, 2, faint),
+    text('SECTOR MAP ROUTE CHOICES', 930, 29, 2, faint),
+  ];
+  const headings = ['0 START', '1', '2', '3', '4', '5 REST', '6 BOSS'];
+  for (const [i, label] of headings.entries()) {
+    elements.push(text(label, originX + columnStep * i, 98, 2, ink, 'center'));
+  }
+  elements.push(panel(before, 128, `BEFORE / MAIN ${base.slice(0, 8).toUpperCase()}`));
+  elements.push(panel(after, 350, 'AFTER / ISSUE 1176 WORKING TREE'));
+  for (const [i, [label, color]] of [['SINGLE EXIT', single], ['CHOICE', choice], ['SAME-TYPE FORK', repeat]].entries()) {
+    const x = 34 + i * 234;
+    elements.push(rect(x, 580, 28, 3, 1, color));
+    elements.push(text(label, x + 38, 570, 2, ink));
+  }
+  return `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="1280" height="700">\n${elements.join('\n')}\n</svg>\n`;
+}
+
+const [, , output, ref = 'origin/main'] = process.argv;
+if (!output) throw new Error('Usage: node draw_maps.js <output-directory> [before-revision]');
+const base = git('rev-parse', '--verify', `${ref}^{commit}`).toString().trim();
+const temp = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'voidsworn-route-before-'));
+try {
+  for (const file of [...FILES, 'ui3d.js']) fs.writeFileSync(path.join(temp, file), git('show', `${base}:${file}`));
+  const before = loadMaps(temp);
+  const after = loadMaps(repo);
+  fs.mkdirSync(output, { recursive: true });
+  for (const [index, seed] of examples.entries()) {
+    const name = `route-1176-${String(seed).padStart(2, '0')}.svg`;
+    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(output, name), diagram(seed, base, before[index], after[index]));
+  }
+} finally {
+  for (const file of [...FILES, 'ui3d.js']) fs.rmSync(path.join(temp, file), { force: true });
+  fs.rmdirSync(temp);
+}

 # Campaign test record (G3)
 
 The evidence behind "G3 campaign rules: done, with parity". Every run below can be repeated with the command next to it.
-Newest first. Each row names the commit the run used and where its log lives.
+Newest first. Each row names the source revision the run used and where its evidence lives.
 
 ## What the oracle is
 
-- The game's own JS campaign files, pinned unchanged: `godot/campaign/test/oracle/`, copied from **8e81d75** (Nov 1 2026: stream B step 9b, the RAMP sector 2 budget, the last rules change; before that f770e06, steps 10 to 12, the lore round 8 strings and the campaign battle-start fix; before that d8b47c6, steps 0 to 4, with one local comment fix). See `oracle/README.md`.
-- The campaign rules in Godot last changed at **ae6073b** (port of stream B step 4, oracle d8b47c6). Step 9b is a number change inside the RAMP table only, so it reaches Godot through the re-exported tables with no code change. Everything after that in `godot/campaign/` is the test harness or notes; the screens (`godot/ui/campaign/`) never change campaign state (every button calls `campaign.act()`), so map and panel work cannot move parity.
+- The Node harness reads the game's own JS campaign files at the repo root. The Godot harness uses `campaign.gd`. The older rows below name the pinned oracle each used at the time.
 - The tables (`data/campaign_tables.json`) are exported from the oracle by `node/export_campaign.js`; every parity run first re-exports them and checks the checksum.

+extends SceneTree
+
+const CampaignScript = preload("res://campaign/campaign.gd")
+const JSM = preload("res://rules/battle_api.gd").JSM
+const SECTORS := 3000
+const REST_TYPES := ["shipyard", "drydock", "field"]
+
+func _init() -> void:
+	var campaign = CampaignScript.new()
+	var err: String = campaign.load_tables()
+	if not err.is_empty():
+		printerr(err)
+		quit(1)
+		return
+	var eligible := 0
+	var two_exits := 0
+	var same_type := 0
+	var first_repeat := ""
+	for seed_value in range(1, SECTORS + 1):
+		var sector := 1 + (seed_value - 1) % 3
+		JSM.rnd_seed(seed_value)
+		var map: Dictionary = campaign.genSector(sector)
+		var problem := layout_problem(map)
+		if not problem.is_empty():
+			printerr("FAIL: seed %d, sector %d: %s" % [seed_value, sector, problem])
+			quit(1)
+			return
+		var types_by_id := {}
+		for col in map["cols"]:
+			for stop in col: types_by_id[stop["id"]] = stop["type"]
+		var next_types := {}
+		for edge in map["edges"]:
+			if not next_types.has(edge[0]): next_types[edge[0]] = []
+			next_types[edge[0]].append(types_by_id[edge[1]])
+		for col in range(5):
+			for stop in map["cols"][col]:
+				var exits: Array = next_types[stop["id"]]
+				if col > 0:
+					eligible += 1
+					if exits.size() == 2: two_exits += 1
+				if exits.size() == 2 and exits[0] == exits[1]:
+					same_type += 1
+					if first_repeat.is_empty():
+						first_repeat = "seed %d, stop %s, type %s" % [seed_value, stop["id"], exits[0]]
+	var rate := 100.0 * two_exits / eligible
+	print("Sector routes: %d sectors, %d/%d stops in columns 1 to 4 with two exits (%.2f%%), %d same-type forks" % [SECTORS, two_exits, eligible, rate, same_type])
+	if same_type > 0: printerr("First same-type fork: " + first_repeat)
+	var passed := rate >= 80.0 and same_type == 0
+	print("PASS: sector route choices" if passed else "FAIL: expected at least 80% two-exit stops and zero same-type forks")
+	quit(0 if passed else 1)
+
+func layout_problem(map: Dictionary) -> String:
+	var cols: Array = map["cols"]
+	if cols.size() != 7 or cols[0].size() != 1 or cols[6].size() != 1 or cols[6][0]["type"] != "boss":
+		return "expected seven columns with a single start and a single boss at column 6"
+	if cols[5].is_empty() or not cols[5].all(func(stop): return stop["type"] in REST_TYPES):
+		return "expected only rest stops in column 5"
+	if not cols[5].any(func(stop): return stop["type"] in ["shipyard", "drydock"]):
+		return "expected a shipyard or drydock in column 5"
+	var columns := {}
+	for col in cols:
+		for stop in col: columns[stop["id"]] = stop["col"]
+	var incoming := {}
+	var outgoing := {}
+	for edge in map["edges"]:
+		var source: String = edge[0]
+		var target: String = edge[1]
+		if not columns.has(source) or not columns.has(target) or columns[target] != columns[source] + 1:
+			return "edge %s to %s must join consecutive columns" % [source, target]
+		if not outgoing.has(source): outgoing[source] = {}
+		if outgoing[source].has(target): return "duplicate edge %s to %s" % [source, target]
+		outgoing[source][target] = true
+		incoming[target] = true
+	for id in columns:
+		if columns[id] > 0 and not incoming.has(id): return "unreachable stop " + id
+		if columns[id] < 6 and not outgoing.has(id): return "dead end at " + id
+	return ""

# Thread test for the rules API (battle_api.gd, "Threads" in API.md). Plays a scenario twice through the API with the same simple
# player bot as api_smoke.gd: once on one thread, once with every enemy step() on a worker thread while the main thread keeps calling
# view(), ui_info(), threat_map(), compute_reach() and preview_fire(), as the battle scene does. Both runs must end with the same
# battle state (JSM canonical hash) and the same events, with no script errors and no halt.
#   godot --headless --path godot --script res://rules/test/thread_test.gd -- [scenario ...]
extends SceneTree
# The JS port keeps JS shapes (untyped Dictionaries), so these warnings are expected here and silenced for this file only.
@warning_ignore_start("inference_on_variant", "untyped_declaration", "unsafe_property_access", "unsafe_method_access", "unsafe_cast", "unsafe_call_argument", "shadowed_variable", "unused_variable", "unused_parameter", "confusable_local_declaration", "confusable_local_usage", "return_value_discarded", "incompatible_ternary")
const API = preload("res://rules/battle_api.gd")
const JSM = preload("res://rules/jsm.gd")

func play(name: String, threaded: bool) -> Dictionary:
	var api = API.new(); var err = api.load_rules()
	if err != "": return {"err": err}
	api.hard_stop = true
	api.new_battle_scenario(name)
	var log := []; var guard := 0; var reads := 0
	while api.phase != "over" and api.phase != "halted" and guard < 2000:
		guard += 1
		var evs := []
		if api.phase == "player":
			var v = api.view(); var me = null
			for u in v.units:
				if u.side == "player" and not u.dead and not u.activated and not u.squadron: me = u; break
			if me == null: api.end_activation(); continue
			var states = api.reach(me.id)
			if states.size() > 1: api.move(me.id, states[states.size() / 2])
			for w in me.weapons:
				for t in v.units:
					if t.side == "enemy" and not t.dead and api.can_fire(me.id, w.i, t.id):
						api.fire(me.id, w.i, t.id); break
			if api.phase == "player": api.end_activation(me.id)
			evs = api.take_events()
		elif not threaded:
			evs = api.step()
		else:
			var box := {"ev": []}
			var tid := WorkerThreadPool.add_task(func() -> void: box["ev"] = api.step())
			while not WorkerThreadPool.is_task_completed(tid):   # the scene's frame loop: read while the enemy thinks
				var v = api.view(); reads += 1
				for u in v.units:
					if not u.dead and u.side == "player":
						api.ui_info(u.id); api.compute_reach(u.id)
						for t in v.units:
							if t.side == "enemy" and not t.dead and u.weapons.size() > 0: api.preview_fire(u.id, 0, t.id); break
						break
				api.threat_map()
			WorkerThreadPool.wait_for_task_completion(tid)
			evs = box["ev"]
		for e in evs: log.append(JSON.stringify(e))
	var st = JSM.sha256(JSM.canon(api.view(), "", {}, true) + JSM.canon(api.R.B.get("log", []), "", {}, true))
	return {"phase": api.phase, "halt": api.halted, "hash": st, "events": log, "reads": reads, "round": api.R.B["round"]}

# Hard stop: a NOT PORTED rule or a rules script error halts the battle and blocks commands until the next new_battle.
func halt_check() -> int:
	var api = API.new(); api.load_rules(); api.hard_stop = true
	api.new_battle_scenario("s1")
	var bad := 0
	api.R.np("thread_test fake rule"); var v = api.view()
	var hev = api.step().filter(func(e): return e.t == "halt")
	if api.phase != "halted" or api.view().halt == null or hev.size() != 1: bad += 1
	var me = null
	for u in v.units:
		if u.side == "player" and not u.dead: me = u; break
	api.end_activation(me.id)
	if api.rotate(me.id, 1) or api.phase != "halted": bad += 1
	api.new_battle_scenario("s1")
	if api.phase == "halted" or api.halted != "": bad += 1
	api.R.not_ported = ""
	var run = {"admiral": "vex", "fac": "voidsworn", "credits": 0, "cpCap": 40, "sector": 1, "relics": [], "inventory": [], "fleet": [api.R.newShip("warden", false)], "boons": [], "patrons": [], "stats": {"battles": 0, "elites": 0, "bosses": 0, "kills": 0}, "fallen": []}
	run["flagUid"] = run["fleet"][0]["uid"]
	api.new_battle(run, {"kind": {"bad": 1}, "fac": "hegemony", "col": 3, "seed": 5, "obj": "rout", "shape": "open", "map": "open", "dep": "line"})
	if api.phase != "halted" or api.cfg_errors.size() != 1: bad += 1
	api.new_battle(run, {"kind": "battle", "seed": 5})
	if api.phase != "halted" or not api.halted.contains("fac"): bad += 1
	print("hard stop check: ", "PASS" if bad == 0 else "FAIL (%d)" % bad, " (", api.halted.left(90), ")")
	return bad

func _init() -> void:
	var names := OS.get_cmdline_user_args()
	if names.is_empty(): names = PackedStringArray(["s4", "s23"])
	var bad := halt_check()
	for name in names:
		var a := play(name, false); var b := play(name, true)
		if a.has("err") or b.has("err"):
			print("thread test ", name, ": FAIL ", a.get("err", b.get("err"))); bad += 1; continue
		var same: bool = a.hash == b.hash and a.events == b.events and a.phase == b.phase
		var ok: bool = same and a.halt == "" and b.halt == ""
		print("thread test %s: %s (%s after round %d, %d events, %d reads during enemy steps on a worker%s)" % [name, "PASS" if ok else "FAIL", b.phase, int(b.round), b.events.size(), b.reads, "" if same else "; states differ" if a.hash != b.hash else "; events differ"])
		if a.halt != "" or b.halt != "": print("  halted: ", a.halt, " | ", b.halt)
		if not ok: bad += 1
	quit(0 if bad == 0 else 1)

  }
