async function run(names = process.argv.slice(2)) {
  if (process.versions.node.split('.')[0] !== '22') throw new Error('Node major 22 is required; use voidsworn_test/node/node.exe');
  const count = positive('PARITY_WORKERS', 4), seconds = positive('PARITY_TIMEOUT_SECONDS', 1200);
  const retrySeconds = positive('PARITY_RETRY_SECONDS', 300);
  if (!names.length) names = Object.keys(S);
  if (new Set(names).size !== names.length || names.some(n => !Object.hasOwn(S, n) || !/^[a-zA-Z0-9_]+$/.test(n))) throw new Error('unknown or duplicate battle name');
  return withRunRoot(dir => {
    root = dir;
    failures = 0;
    return runChecks(names, count, seconds, retrySeconds);
  });
}
async function runChecks(names, count, seconds, retrySeconds) {
  for (const dir of ['golden', 'out', 'workers']) fs.mkdirSync(path.join(root, dir));
  const env = isolated(path.join(root, 'user')), started = Date.now();
  say(`Voidsworn rules parity: ${names.length} battles, ${Math.min(count, names.length)} workers`);
  say(`Started (UTC): ${new Date(started).toISOString()}; Node ${process.version}; Godot ${godot}`);
  say(`Records and log: ${root}`);
  say('[1/5] Exporting the game data tables for Godot...');
  checked('data export', cleanExit(await js('export_data.js', [path.join(P, 'rules/data/tables.json')], path.join(root, 'export.log'))));
  say('[2/5] Random numbers: first 10,000 draws from 3 seeds...');
  for (const seed of ['1001', '7', '4242424242']) {
    const a = path.join(root, 'golden', `prng_${seed}.txt`), b = path.join(root, 'out', `prng_${seed}.txt`), log = b + '.compare.txt';
    const n = await js('golden.js', ['prng', seed, '10000', a], a + '.log');
    const g = await gd(['prng', seed, '10000', b], b + '.log', env);
    const same = await compare('same', a, b, log);
    checked(`seed ${seed}`, cleanExit(n) && cleanExit(g) && same);
    if (!same) say(read(log).trim());
  }
  say('[3/5] Math functions on all 316,090 inputs...');
  const math = path.join(root, 'golden/math.txt'), mathLog = path.join(root, 'out/math.txt');
  const mn = await js('golden.js', ['math', math], math + '.log'), mg = await gd(['math', math], mathLog, env);
  checked('math', cleanExit(mn) && cleanExit(mg), read(mathLog).split('\n').find(l => l.startsWith('math ')) || 'see out/math.txt');
  say('[4/5] Seeded battles, every action compared (state hash, PRNG state)...');
  const manifest = { names, retrySeconds, workers: [] };
  for (let id = 0; id < Math.min(count, names.length); id++) {
    const list = names.filter((_, i) => i % Math.min(count, names.length) === id);
    const file = path.join(root, 'workers', `part${id}.json`), log = path.join(root, 'workers', `godot${id}.log`);
    fs.writeFileSync(file, JSON.stringify(Object.fromEntries(list.map(n => [n, S[n]]))));
    manifest.workers.push({ id, names: list, file, log, browser: {} });
  }
  await waitForAll(manifest.workers.map(async w => {
    const browser = async () => {
      for (const name of w.names) w.browser[name] = await js('golden.js', ['scenario', name, path.join(root, 'golden', name + '.jsonl')], path.join(root, 'workers', `browser_${name}.log`));
    };
    const env = isolated(path.join(root, 'workers', `user${w.id}`));
    await waitForAll([browser(), gd(['batch', w.file, path.join(root, 'out')], w.log, env, seconds, true).then(r => { w.godot = r; })]);
    say(`  worker ${w.id}: batch ended (${w.names.length} assigned battles; Godot exit ${result(w.godot)})`);
  }));
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(root, 'batches.json'), JSON.stringify(manifest, null, 2));
  await verifyBattles(manifest);
  say('[4b] Threat overlay, rounds 1 to 3...');
  for (const name of ['s4', 's19', 's23']) {
    const a = path.join(root, 'golden', `threat_${name}.json`), b = path.join(root, 'out', `threat_${name}.json`);
    const n = await js('golden.js', ['threat', name, a], a + '.log'), g = await gd(['threat', name, b], b + '.log', env);
    checked(`threat map ${name}`, cleanExit(n) && cleanExit(g) && present(a) && present(b) && fs.readFileSync(a).equals(fs.readFileSync(b)));
  }
  say('[4c] Threads and the hard stop (s4, s23)...');
  const threadLog = path.join(root, 'out/thread.txt');
  const thread = await execute(godot, ['--headless', '--path', P, '--script', 'res://rules/test/thread_test.gd', '--', 's4', 's23'], threadLog, env);
  checked('threads', cleanExit(thread));
  say(read(threadLog).split('\n').filter(l => /^(thread test|hard stop|  halted)/.test(l)).join('\n'));
  say('[5/5] Enemy think time (report only; game bar: worst under about 0.5 s)...');
  for (const name of names.filter(n => S[n].timing)) {
    const a = path.join(root, 'golden', name + '.jsonl.timing.json'), b = path.join(root, 'out', name + '.jsonl.timing.json');
    if (!present(a) || !present(b)) { checked(`timing records ${name}`, false, 'missing file'); continue; }
    const log = path.join(root, 'out', 'timing_' + name + '.txt'), r = await js('timing.js', [name, a, b], log);
    say(read(log).trim());
    if (!cleanExit(r)) checked(`timing report ${name}`, false);
  }
  // Path D step 3: use these very same Godot records. This never replaces a parity comparison.
  const parityFailures = failures;
  say('[saved] Checking Godot results against the reviewed results in the repo...');
  const saved = require('../saved/check').check(root, { names, print: say });
  failures += saved.failures;
  const elapsed = (Date.now() - started) / 1000;
  say(`Whole rules parity step: ${elapsed.toFixed(3)} s`);
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(root, 'summary.json'), JSON.stringify({ battles: names.length, workers: manifest.workers.length, failures, parity_failures: parityFailures, saved_failures: saved.failures, saved_seconds: saved.seconds, started_at: new Date(started).toISOString(), completed_at: new Date().toISOString(), seconds: elapsed }, null, 2));
  if (process.env.GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY) fs.appendFileSync(process.env.GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY, `\nWhole rules parity and saved-results step: ${elapsed.toFixed(3)} s (saved comparison: ${saved.seconds.toFixed(3)} s).\n`);
  say(failures ? `FAIL: ${failures} check(s) differ.` : "PASS: the Godot port matches the game's rules exactly.");
  return failures ? 1 : 0;
}
// Recheck saved batch evidence using the same comparison and missing-file diagnostics.
async function verify(savedRoot) {
  root = path.resolve(savedRoot); failures = 0;
  await verifyBattles(JSON.parse(read(path.join(root, 'batches.json'))));
  return failures ? 1 : 0;
}
module.exports = { run, verify, execute, isolated, withRunRoot };

'use strict';
const fs = require('node:fs'), path = require('node:path');
const R = require('./records');
function check(root, { names, saved = R.HOME, scenarios = R.SCENARIOS, print = console.log } = {}) {
  const start = Date.now(), s = R.json(scenarios);
  names ||= Object.keys(s);
  const lines = ['Saved Godot results: changed battles', 'Later differences count changed records after the first, aligned by turn index.'];
  let failures = 0, changed = 0, filesChanged = 0;
  const fail = (label, detail) => { failures++; lines.push(`FAIL ${label}`, ...detail.map(l => '  ' + l)); };
  let m;
  try { m = R.manifest(saved); } catch (e) { fail('saved results', [e.message]); }
  if (m) {
    let bad;
    try { bad = R.evidence(root, names, s); } catch (e) { bad = Object.fromEntries(names.map(n => [n, e.message])); }
    if (names.length === Object.keys(s).length) {
      for (const n of Object.keys(m.battles).filter(n => !Object.hasOwn(s, n))) fail('battle ' + n, ['removed from scenarios; update the saved results in this pull request']);
    }
    for (const name of names) {
      try {
        if (bad[name]) throw Error(bad[name]);
        const bytes = R.read(path.join(root, 'out', name + '.jsonl'));
        const rows = R.records(bytes), entry = m.battles[name];
        if (!entry) { changed++; fail('battle ' + name, ['new battle; no saved result']); continue; }
        const before = R.unpack(saved, entry);
        if (entry.records !== R.records(before).length) throw Error('saved record count differs from manifest');
        if (R.sha(bytes) !== entry.sha256) { changed++; fail('battle ' + name, R.battleDiff(before, bytes)); }
        else if (entry.scenario_sha256 !== R.sha(R.stable(s[name]))) fail('battle ' + name, ['scenario changed but result is identical; update its saved input hash']);
        if (!rows.length) throw Error('no records');
      } catch (e) { fail('battle ' + name, [e.message]); }
    }
    for (const name of R.FILES) {
      try {
        const bytes = R.auxiliary(root, name), before = R.unpack(saved, m.files[name]);
        if (R.sha(bytes) !== m.files[name].sha256) {
          filesChanged++;
          let detail;
          if (name.startsWith('threat_')) {
            const d = R.diff(JSON.parse(before), JSON.parse(bytes), 'map');
            detail = d ? `${d.field}: ${R.value(d.before)} -> ${R.value(d.after)}` : 'bytes changed; map values identical';
          } else {
            const a = before.toString().split('\n'), b = bytes.toString().split('\n');
            const i = Array.from({ length: Math.max(a.length, b.length) }, (_, j) => j).find(j => a[j] !== b[j]);
            detail = `line ${i + 1}: ${R.value(a[i])} -> ${R.value(b[i])}`;
          }
          fail('file ' + name, [detail]);
        }
      } catch (e) { fail('file ' + name, [e.message]); }
    }
  }
  const seconds = (Date.now() - start) / 1000;
  lines.push(`${failures ? 'FAIL' : 'PASS'}: saved Godot results; ${names.length} battles checked, ${changed} changed, ${filesChanged} other files changed, ${failures} failures; ${seconds.toFixed(3)} s`);
  const report = path.join(root, 'changed-battles.txt');
  fs.writeFileSync(report, lines.join('\n') + '\n');
  const summary = { battles: names.length, changed, files_changed: filesChanged, failures, seconds };
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(root, 'saved_summary.json'), JSON.stringify(summary, null, 2) + '\n');
  print(lines.join('\n')); print('Changed-battles report: ' + report);
  if (process.env.GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY) fs.appendFileSync(process.env.GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY,
    '\n### Saved Godot results\n\n```text\n' + lines.join('\n') + '\n```\n');
  return summary;
}
function options(args) {
  const o = {};
  for (let i = 0; i < args.length; i++) {
    if (!['--from', '--saved', '--scenarios'].includes(args[i]) || !args[i + 1]) throw Error('usage: check.js [--from <parity run>] [--saved <saved folder>] [--scenarios <file>]');
    o[args[i].slice(2)] = path.resolve(args[++i]);
  }
  return o;
}

// Require the original batch evidence, not a diagnostic retry or an old loose file.
function evidence(root, names, scenarios) {
  const batch = json(path.join(root, 'batches.json'));
  if (!Array.isArray(batch.names) || stable([...batch.names].sort()) !== stable([...names].sort()))
    throw Error('run battle list differs from the requested list');
  const seen = new Set(), errors = {};
  for (const w of batch.workers) {
    const log = fs.readFileSync(w.log, 'utf8').replace(/\r\n/g, '\n');
    const part = json(w.file);
    const ok = w.godot?.code === 0 && !w.godot.timeout && !w.godot.error && !w.godot.signal &&
      log.split('\n').includes(`batch ${w.names.length} battles -> ${path.join(root, 'out')}`) &&
      !/SCRIPT ERROR|Parse Error|NOT PORTED:|^ERROR:/m.test(log);
    if (stable(Object.keys(part).sort()) !== stable([...w.names].sort())) throw Error('worker partition differs from its manifest');
    for (const name of w.names) {
      if (!names.includes(name) || seen.has(name)) throw Error('unknown or duplicate battle in workers: ' + name);
      seen.add(name);
      if (stable(part[name]) !== stable(scenarios[name])) errors[name] = 'run used a different scenario; run again';
      else if (!ok || !log.split('\n').includes(`batch result ${name} exit 0`)) errors[name] = 'original Godot batch failed or did not finish';
    }
  }
  for (const name of names) if (!seen.has(name)) errors[name] = 'not assigned to a Godot worker';
  return errors;
}

function auxiliary(root, name) {
  if (name === 'math.txt') return read(path.join(root, 'golden', name));
  if (name === 'math_verdict.txt') {
    const lines = read(path.join(root, 'out/math.txt')).toString().replace(/\r/g, '').split('\n');
    const verdict = lines.filter(l => /^math \d+ values, differences from Node: /.test(l));
    const count = read(path.join(root, 'golden/math.txt')).toString().trim().split('\n').length;
    if (verdict.length !== 1 || verdict[0] !== `math ${count} values, differences from Node: none`)
      throw Error('Godot did not verify every math value');
    return Buffer.from(verdict[0] + '\n');
  }
  const bytes = read(path.join(root, 'out', name));
  if (name.startsWith('prng_') && bytes.toString().trim().split('\n').length !== 10000)
    throw Error('incomplete PRNG file');
  if (name.startsWith('threat_')) {
    const value = JSON.parse(bytes);
    if (stable(Object.keys(value)) !== stable(['1', '2', '3']) || Object.values(value).some(v => !Object.keys(v).length))
      throw Error('incomplete threat map');
  }
  return bytes;
}
function diff(a, b, field = 'state') {
  if (Object.is(a, b)) return null;
  if (a && b && typeof a === 'object' && typeof b === 'object' && Array.isArray(a) === Array.isArray(b)) {
    const keys = Array.isArray(a) ? Array.from({ length: Math.max(a.length, b.length) }, (_, i) => i) :
      [...new Set([...Object.keys(a), ...Object.keys(b)])].sort();
    for (const k of keys) {
      const d = diff(a[k], b[k], field + (Array.isArray(a) ? `[${k}]` : '.' + k));
      if (d) return d;
    }
    return null;
  }
  return { field, before: a, after: b };
}
function value(v) {
  if (v === undefined) return '<missing>';
  if (typeof v === 'string' && /^d:[0-9a-f]{16}$/.test(v)) return `${Buffer.from(v.slice(2), 'hex').readDoubleBE()} [${v}]`;
  const s = JSON.stringify(v);
  return s.length > 180 ? s.slice(0, 177) + '...' : s;
}
function battleDiff(beforeBytes, afterBytes) {
  const a = records(beforeBytes), b = records(afterBytes);
  const changed = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < Math.max(a.length, b.length); i++) {
    const x = a[i], y = b[i];
    if (!x || !y || ['hash', 'rng', 'turn', 'round', 'actor'].some(k => x[k] !== y[k])) changed.push(i);
  }
  if (!changed.length) return ['record bytes/format changed; battle values identical; later changed records: 0'];

+# Sector map route choices
+
+Every stop in columns 1 to 4 offers two routes to different types of stops. The opening stop links to every stop in column 1. Column 5 offers rest stops, and every rest stop leads to the boss in column 6.
+
+The check generated 3,000 sectors with seeds 1 to 3,000, rotating through sectors 1, 2 and 3. It counted stops with exactly two exits in columns 1 to 4 and checked every two-way fork for repeated stop types, including forks at the starting stop.
+
+| Source | Two-exit stops, columns 1 to 4 | Same-type forks |
+|---|---:|---:|
+| Before, main `da81dd82` | 14,778 of 35,905 (41.16%) | 3,463 |
+| After, issue 1176 | 35,905 of 35,905 (100%) | 0 |
+
+The brief cites 58.7% for main. In this sample, 21,127 stops (58.84%) on main had one exit. All stops remained reachable in both samples. The JavaScript and Godot versions produced identical stop types and routes for all 3,000 new maps.
+
+Campaign parity passed for seeds 1 through 30, and the first 40 steps of seed 1 matched in the separate save hand-off check. The [campaign parity record](../../../godot/campaign/test/RECORD.md) has the table checksum and save counts.
+
+## Before and after
+
+Each diagram shows the same seed and sector before and after the change. The labels on the stops give their types. Red lines mark repeated types at a two-way fork. Blue lines mark choices.
+
+![ROUTE-1176-01, sector 1, seed 1](../issue-1176-codex/route-1176-01.png)
+
+![ROUTE-1176-02, sector 2, seed 2](../issue-1176-codex/route-1176-02.png)
+
+![ROUTE-1176-03, sector 3, seed 3](../issue-1176-codex/route-1176-03.png)
+
+Run `node docs/shots/route_choice_1176/draw_maps.js <scratch-directory> da81dd82` from the repo root to redraw the SVG diagrams. To rerun the sector check, use `godot --headless --path godot --script res://campaign/test/sector_routes_test.gd`.

+'use strict';
+
+// node draw_maps.js <output-directory> [before-revision]
+// Draws the route diagrams as SVG. The review copies are rasterized and stamped separately.
+const fs = require('node:fs');
+const os = require('node:os');
+const path = require('node:path');
+const { execFileSync } = require('node:child_process');
+
+const repo = path.resolve(__dirname, '../../..');
+const harness = path.join(repo, 'godot/campaign/test/node/env.js');
+const { FILES } = require(harness);
+const font = require(path.join(repo, 'tools/picture_font.json'));
+const examples = [1, 2, 3];
+const colors = {
+  battle: '#e6ba8c', elite: '#f69082', event: '#b3a7ef',
+  shipyard: '#8fd9c4', drydock: '#82cfe9', field: '#d5db98', boss: '#f7af6b',
+};
+const originX = 112;
+const columnStep = 176;
+const ink = '#edf0f7';
+const faint = '#a8b6cd';
+const single = '#627897';
+const choice = '#79caff';
+const repeat = '#fc9f87';
+
+function git(...args) {
+  return execFileSync('git', ['-C', repo, ...args]);
+}
+
+function loadMaps(oracle) {
+  const sample = `
+    function sampleMap(seed) {
+      rndSeed(seed);
+      return genSector(1 + (seed - 1) % 3);
+    }
+    console.log(JSON.stringify(${JSON.stringify(examples)}.map(sampleMap)));
+  `;
+  const loader = 'require(process.argv[1]).load(process.argv[2])';
+  return JSON.parse(execFileSync(process.execPath, ['-e', loader, harness, sample], {
+    env: { ...process.env, VS_ORACLE: oracle },
+  }));
+}
+
+function text(label, x, y, scale, fill, align = 'left') {
+  // The bundled bitmap font becomes paths, so a CPU SVG rasterizer needs no fonts.
+  if (align === 'center') x -= label.length * 3 * scale;
+  let pixels = '';
+  for (const [index, letter] of [...label].entries()) {
+    const glyph = font[letter] || font['?'];
+    for (const [row, bits] of glyph.entries()) {
+      for (let col = 0; col < 6; col++) {
+        if (bits & (1 << (5 - col))) pixels += `M${index * 6 + col} ${row}h1v1h-1z`;
+      }
+    }
+  }
+  return `<path d="${pixels}" transform="translate(${x} ${y}) scale(${scale})" fill="${fill}"/>`;
+}
+
+function rect(x, y, w, h, radius, fill, stroke = 'none') {
+  return `<rect x="${x}" y="${y}" width="${w}" height="${h}" rx="${radius}" fill="${fill}" stroke="${stroke}"/>`;
+}
+
+function panel(map, top, label) {
+  const stops = new Map(map.cols.flat().map(stop => [stop.id, stop]));
+  const exits = new Map([...stops.keys()].map(id => [id, []]));
+  for (const [from, to] of map.edges) exits.get(from).push(stops.get(to));
+  const eligible = map.cols.slice(1, 5).flat();
+  const two = eligible.filter(stop => exits.get(stop.id).length === 2).length;
+  const sameType = id => {
+    const next = exits.get(id);
+    return next.length === 2 && next[0].type === next[1].type;
+  };
+  const repeated = [...stops.keys()].filter(sameType).length;
+  const y = stop => Math.round(top + 49 + 142 * stop.y);
+  const x = stop => originX + columnStep * stop.col;
+  const elements = [
+    rect(32, top, 1216, 198, 16, '#141f32', '#2a3a55'),
+    text(label, 51, top + 14, 2, ink),
+    text(`${two}/${eligible.length} TWO EXITS IN COLS 1-4   |   SAME-TYPE FORKS: ${repeated}`, 580, top + 14, 2, faint),
+  ];
+  for (const [from, to] of map.edges) {
+    const a = stops.get(from), b = stops.get(to);
+    const edgeColor = sameType(from) ? repeat : exits.get(from).length >= 2 ? choice : single;
+    elements.push(`<path d="M${x(a) + 51} ${y(a)}C${x(a) + 90} ${y(a)} ${x(b) - 90} ${y(b)} ${x(b) - 51} ${y(b)}" fill="none" stroke="${edgeColor}" stroke-width="2"/>`);
+  }
+  for (const stop of stops.values()) {
+    const color = colors[stop.type];
+    elements.push(rect(x(stop) - 51, y(stop) - 14, 102, 28, 7, '#101827', color));
+    elements.push(text(stop.type.toUpperCase(), x(stop), y(stop) - 11, 2, color, 'center'));
+  }
+  return elements.join('\n');
+}
+
+function diagram(seed, base, before, after) {
+  const code = `ROUTE-1176-${String(seed).padStart(2, '0')}`;
+  const elements = [
+    rect(0, 0, 1280, 700, 0, '#0c1423'),
+    text(code, 34, 24, 3, ink),
+    text(`SECTOR ${seed}   |   SEED ${seed}`, 34, 62, 2, faint),
+    text('SECTOR MAP ROUTE CHOICES', 930, 29, 2, faint),
+  ];
+  const headings = ['0 START', '1', '2', '3', '4', '5 REST', '6 BOSS'];
+  for (const [i, label] of headings.entries()) {
+    elements.push(text(label, originX + columnStep * i, 98, 2, ink, 'center'));
+  }
+  elements.push(panel(before, 128, `BEFORE / MAIN ${base.slice(0, 8).toUpperCase()}`));
+  elements.push(panel(after, 350, 'AFTER / ISSUE 1176 WORKING TREE'));
+  for (const [i, [label, color]] of [['SINGLE EXIT', single], ['CHOICE', choice], ['SAME-TYPE FORK', repeat]].entries()) {
+    const x = 34 + i * 234;
+    elements.push(rect(x, 580, 28, 3, 1, color));
+    elements.push(text(label, x + 38, 570, 2, ink));
+  }
+  return `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="1280" height="700">\n${elements.join('\n')}\n</svg>\n`;
+}
+
+const [, , output, ref = 'origin/main'] = process.argv;
+if (!output) throw new Error('Usage: node draw_maps.js <output-directory> [before-revision]');
+const base = git('rev-parse', '--verify', `${ref}^{commit}`).toString().trim();
+const temp = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'voidsworn-route-before-'));
+try {
+  for (const file of [...FILES, 'ui3d.js']) fs.writeFileSync(path.join(temp, file), git('show', `${base}:${file}`));
+  const before = loadMaps(temp);
+  const after = loadMaps(repo);
+  fs.mkdirSync(output, { recursive: true });
+  for (const [index, seed] of examples.entries()) {
+    const name = `route-1176-${String(seed).padStart(2, '0')}.svg`;
+    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(output, name), diagram(seed, base, before[index], after[index]));
+  }
+} finally {
+  for (const file of [...FILES, 'ui3d.js']) fs.rmSync(path.join(temp, file), { force: true });
+  fs.rmdirSync(temp);
+}

 # Campaign test record (G3)
 
 The evidence behind "G3 campaign rules: done, with parity". Every run below can be repeated with the command next to it.
-Newest first. Each row names the commit the run used and where its log lives.
+Newest first. Each row names the source revision the run used and where its evidence lives.
 
 ## What the oracle is
 
-- The game's own JS campaign files, pinned unchanged: `godot/campaign/test/oracle/`, copied from **8e81d75** (Nov 1 2026: stream B step 9b, the RAMP sector 2 budget, the last rules change; before that f770e06, steps 10 to 12, the lore round 8 strings and the campaign battle-start fix; before that d8b47c6, steps 0 to 4, with one local comment fix). See `oracle/README.md`.
-- The campaign rules in Godot last changed at **ae6073b** (port of stream B step 4, oracle d8b47c6). Step 9b is a number change inside the RAMP table only, so it reaches Godot through the re-exported tables with no code change. Everything after that in `godot/campaign/` is the test harness or notes; the screens (`godot/ui/campaign/`) never change campaign state (every button calls `campaign.act()`), so map and panel work cannot move parity.
+- The Node harness reads the game's own JS campaign files at the repo root. The Godot harness uses `campaign.gd`. The older rows below name the pinned oracle each used at the time.
 - The tables (`data/campaign_tables.json`) are exported from the oracle by `node/export_campaign.js`; every parity run first re-exports them and checks the checksum.

+extends SceneTree
+
+const CampaignScript = preload("res://campaign/campaign.gd")
+const JSM = preload("res://rules/battle_api.gd").JSM
+const SECTORS := 3000
+const REST_TYPES := ["shipyard", "drydock", "field"]
+
+func _init() -> void:
+	var campaign = CampaignScript.new()
+	var err: String = campaign.load_tables()
+	if not err.is_empty():
+		printerr(err)
+		quit(1)
+		return
+	var eligible := 0
+	var two_exits := 0
+	var same_type := 0
+	var first_repeat := ""
+	for seed_value in range(1, SECTORS + 1):
+		var sector := 1 + (seed_value - 1) % 3
+		JSM.rnd_seed(seed_value)
+		var map: Dictionary = campaign.genSector(sector)
+		var problem := layout_problem(map)
+		if not problem.is_empty():
+			printerr("FAIL: seed %d, sector %d: %s" % [seed_value, sector, problem])
+			quit(1)
+			return
+		var types_by_id := {}
+		for col in map["cols"]:
+			for stop in col: types_by_id[stop["id"]] = stop["type"]
+		var next_types := {}
+		for edge in map["edges"]:
+			if not next_types.has(edge[0]): next_types[edge[0]] = []
+			next_types[edge[0]].append(types_by_id[edge[1]])
+		for col in range(5):
+			for stop in map["cols"][col]:
+				var exits: Array = next_types[stop["id"]]
+				if col > 0:
+					eligible += 1
+					if exits.size() == 2: two_exits += 1
+				if exits.size() == 2 and exits[0] == exits[1]:
+					same_type += 1
+					if first_repeat.is_empty():
+						first_repeat = "seed %d, stop %s, type %s" % [seed_value, stop["id"], exits[0]]
+	var rate := 100.0 * two_exits / eligible
+	print("Sector routes: %d sectors, %d/%d stops in columns 1 to 4 with two exits (%.2f%%), %d same-type forks" % [SECTORS, two_exits, eligible, rate, same_type])
+	if same_type > 0: printerr("First same-type fork: " + first_repeat)
+	var passed := rate >= 80.0 and same_type == 0
+	print("PASS: sector route choices" if passed else "FAIL: expected at least 80% two-exit stops and zero same-type forks")
+	quit(0 if passed else 1)
+
+func layout_problem(map: Dictionary) -> String:
+	var cols: Array = map["cols"]
+	if cols.size() != 7 or cols[0].size() != 1 or cols[6].size() != 1 or cols[6][0]["type"] != "boss":
+		return "expected seven columns with a single start and a single boss at column 6"
+	if cols[5].is_empty() or not cols[5].all(func(stop): return stop["type"] in REST_TYPES):
+		return "expected only rest stops in column 5"
+	if not cols[5].any(func(stop): return stop["type"] in ["shipyard", "drydock"]):
+		return "expected a shipyard or drydock in column 5"
+	var columns := {}
+	for col in cols:
+		for stop in col: columns[stop["id"]] = stop["col"]
+	var incoming := {}
+	var outgoing := {}
+	for edge in map["edges"]:
+		var source: String = edge[0]
+		var target: String = edge[1]
+		if not columns.has(source) or not columns.has(target) or columns[target] != columns[source] + 1:
+			return "edge %s to %s must join consecutive columns" % [source, target]
+		if not outgoing.has(source): outgoing[source] = {}
+		if outgoing[source].has(target): return "duplicate edge %s to %s" % [source, target]
+		outgoing[source][target] = true
+		incoming[target] = true
+	for id in columns:
+		if columns[id] > 0 and not incoming.has(id): return "unreachable stop " + id
+		if columns[id] < 6 and not outgoing.has(id): return "dead end at " + id
+	return ""
